# MusePi Changelog
MusePi 定制版本的发布说明,供启动时的"新功能"面板(`changelog.startup`)与
`/changelog` 展示。上游 oh-my-pi 的变更记录在 `CHANGELOG.md`(本文件存在时
优先读取本文件)。

## [Unreleased]

### Fixed

- **assistant 消息上的重试按钮改为原地重新生成，不再重发用户消息**：此前点重试是把该轮的用户消息重新发一遍——旧回复留在树上成为孤立分支，"重试"语义与直觉不符，还多花一次模型调用。修法：新增 `session.regenerateAt` RPC + `AgentSession.regenerateAt(targetId)`——把会话树切到目标 assistant 节点的父级（叶子移到父节点），随后由 agent 原地继续工作；守护与 branchAt 同口径（进行中/压缩中/目标非 assistant 返回失败，不发广播）。GUI 重试按钮改传本行 assistant 消息的 id（原来传的是用户消息 id），成功后跟随新叶（清 pinned 路径 + pulse），旧回复留在树上作为兄弟分支可回切。4 例新契约测试（视图键解析委托/leaf_moved 广播/busy 时失败且不广播/未知消息）。
  - EN: The retry button on an assistant message now regenerates in place instead of resending the user message. Previously, clicking retry resent that turn's user message — the old reply stayed on the tree as an orphaned branch, the "retry" semantics disagreed with intuition, and it cost an extra model call. Fix: new `session.regenerateAt` RPC + `AgentSession.regenerateAt(targetId)` — the session tree navigates to the target assistant node's parent (the leaf moves to the parent node) and the agent continues working in place; guards share the branchAt rule (in-flight / compacting / non-assistant target returns failure with no broadcast). The GUI retry button now passes the current row's assistant message id (it used to pass the user message id) and follows the new leaf on success (clears the pinned path + pulse); the old reply stays on the tree as a sibling branch you can switch back to. 4 new contract tests (view-key resolution delegation / leaf_moved broadcast / busy failure with no broadcast / unknown message).

- **回退/切分支后概览面只呈现激活路径（轨迹/导航条不再把兄弟分支算进当前会话）**：回退到某节点重新发消息后，主会话转录已按 leaf-walk 过滤，但右侧轨迹的时间线与统计仍按全量 journal 轮次派生——"轮次"把兄弟分支的轮也计进去（实机会话 2 轮显示 6 轮）、轮次列表列出已回退分支的内容，导航条同样索引全会话轮次，层级与在视分支不符。修法：ChatView 新增 pathEntries——与转录同一套 leaf-walk 契约（walk complete 或 daemon 捎带 pinnedPath 才过滤，拓扑不可信回退全量旧行为），轨迹视图的时间线模式与统计改吃路径集（分支树模式保留全量淡显，它本身就是分支结构视图），导航条轮次按路径 entryId 过滤（仅 walk complete 时——pinned 集是尾窗截断，rail 还肩负全量索引 + 向上翻页职责）。7 例新契约测试钉死实机拓扑（会话 01a0f7a7：新分支叶走到真根判 complete、兄弟分支整批离开转录、尾窗断链/非最老无父节点两态一行不藏、pin 根与断链捎带路径两契约）。
  - EN: After a rollback / branch switch, the overview surfaces now show only the active branch path (the trajectory and turn rail no longer count sibling branches into the current session). The main transcript was already filtered by the leaf-walk, but the right-panel trajectory's timeline and stats were still derived from the FULL journal turn set — "turns" counted sibling-branch rounds (a real session with 2 turns showed 6), the turn list showed content from the rolled-back branch, and the rail indexed the whole session's turns, so their hierarchy disagreed with the branch in view. Fix: ChatView gains pathEntries — the same leaf-walk contract as the transcript (filter only when the walk completes or the daemon ships a pinned path; fall back to the full set when the topology is untrusted), the trajectory view's timeline mode and stats consume the path set (tree mode keeps the full set with dimming — it IS the branch-structure view), and the rail's turns are filtered by path entryId (only when the walk completes — the pinned set is a tail-window truncation, and the rail still serves as the full-session index + page-up channel). 7 new contract tests pin the real-session topology (session 01a0f7a7: the new-branch leaf walks to the genuine root and completes, sibling branches leave the transcript en masse, the tail-window chain-cut and non-oldest-parentless states hide nothing, plus the pin-to-root and cut-chain-with-pinned-path contracts).

- **view-store 落盘改增量投影，流式期间不再每 100ms 全表重写（P1-13）**：daemon 的 persist 调度此前每次 upsert 都 DELETE 全表 + 逐条重插 messages + 整体 JSON.stringify 快照——流式期间约每 100ms 跑一次的同步事务，长会话（10⁴ 行 × 10/s）直接阻塞事件循环。修法：MaterializedView 新增 drainDirtyEntries 脏通道（条目产生/内容替换点记账，persist 调度取走交给 ViewStore）；ViewStore 维护 (sessionId → id→seq) 索引并做位置对齐判定——头部 id→seq 位置不变且尾部全是新 id 时只写 changed 行（已知 id 按 seq UPDATE，新 id 在 maxSeq+1 INSERT 并登记），前插/截断/重排失对齐回退全表重建（fail-safe）；materialized_sessions 的 JSON 快照按 1s 节流写（force 可绕过，dispose/压缩路径保持即时落盘）；remove 清理全部增量状态。6 例新契约测试钉死（内容 persist 只写 1 行且检索新鲜/追加只插尾行且 seq 连续/无变更 persist 写 0 行/前插失对齐回退全量且保持一致的/节流跳过与 force 绕过/remove 后复活全量重建）。
  - EN: The view-store now persists incrementally instead of rewriting the whole table every 100ms while streaming (P1-13). The daemon's persist scheduler used to DELETE the entire messages table + re-insert every row + JSON.stringify the whole snapshot on EVERY upsert — a synchronous transaction running roughly every 100ms during streaming, blocking the event loop outright on long sessions (10⁴ rows × 10/s). Fix: MaterializedView gains a drainDirtyEntries dirty channel (booked at every entry creation / content replacement, drained by the persist scheduler and handed to ViewStore); ViewStore keeps a (sessionId → id→seq) index and judges positional alignment — when the head's id→seq mapping is unchanged and the tail is all-new ids, only the changed rows are written (known ids UPDATE by seq, new ids INSERT at maxSeq+1 and are registered), while prepend/truncate/reorder misalignment falls back to a full-table rebuild (fail-safe); the materialized_sessions JSON snapshot is throttled to a 1s write interval (force bypasses; dispose/compaction paths still persist immediately); remove clears all incremental state. 6 new contract tests pin it (a content persist writes exactly 1 row and search stays fresh / an append inserts only the tail rows with contiguous seqs / a no-change persist writes zero rows / prepend misalignment falls back to a full rebuild and stays consistent / throttle skips and force bypasses / remove-then-resurrect rebuilds fully).

- **轨迹/地图全量历史补全不再持内存双份副本，失败可重试（P0-4）**：ChatView 的 ensureFullHistory 此前把 session.history 分页拉回的每条 entry 原文存进第二份本地数组（长会话数千轮下是数百 MB 的重复内容，与 store 的 tail 条目还共享引用），且任一分页 RPC 失败即永久闩锁——无提示、无重试，轨迹/地图静默停留在尾窗局部历史。修法：摄入时按 `OVERVIEW_TEXT_CAP`（400，概览消费方的最大显示截断）预裁——新增 `lightenOverviewEntry`（长文本块/thinking/result/顾问 note 截 400、图片块剥掉 base64 `data` 保留标记），显示输出对所有概览消费方（treeTextOf / treeKindOf / treeVerdictOf / buildMessageTree）恒等，契约测试钉死；stale 游标改走 catch 而非静默 break；catch 复位闩锁并置 `fullError`，轨迹面板渲染显式降级提示（点击直达重试，i18n zh/en）。明细按需仍由 session.history 分页 + requestJump 覆盖，不另做。
  - EN: The trajectory/map full-history backfill no longer keeps a second in-memory copy of every entry, and failures are retryable (P0-4). ChatView's ensureFullHistory used to store every entry returned by the session.history pages verbatim into a second local array (hundreds of MB duplicated for long sessions, sharing references with the store's tail entries), and any single page RPC failure latched permanently — no notice, no retry; trajectory/map silently stayed on the partial tail window. Fix: entries are pre-trimmed at ingest to `OVERVIEW_TEXT_CAP` (400, the largest display truncation across overview consumers) — new `lightenOverviewEntry` (long text/thinking/result/advisor-note blocks capped at 400, image blocks stripped of their base64 `data` but keeping the marker); display output is identical for every overview consumer (treeTextOf / treeKindOf / treeVerdictOf / buildMessageTree), pinned by contract tests; a stale cursor now throws into catch instead of silently breaking; catch resets the latch and sets `fullError`, and the trajectory panel renders an explicit degraded-state notice (click goes straight to retry, i18n zh/en). Details on demand remain covered by session.history paging + requestJump, nothing duplicated.

- **轨迹/地图派生改增量缓存，流式帧重算量与总条目脱钩（P1-11）**：轨迹面板与轮级地图挂在每帧换引用的 store 输出上（snapshot 每帧 spread 新数组、leafWalk 沿 snap.entries 重建出新 Set、roundDurations 每帧新数组），旧实现每个流式帧都对全量历史（全量补全后上万条）跑 buildTrajectoryTree 全量重算——两遍遍历 + 每条的文本截断/参数序列化，长会话下是每帧数百 ms 的热路径；且 depthOf 在父链断裂时跳过记忆化，单次重算内部再退化为 O(n²)（尾窗头部条目父在窗外是常态）。修法：新增 trajectory-derive 增量派生（turn-derive 先例）——按对象身份找公共前缀，前缀事件与运行态断点（轮计数/工具计数/首末 ts/回填索引/事件数）原样复用，只重跑增量片段；activePath 内容变化（GUI 每帧新 Set 引用，同内容视为同路径）/前插/收缩触发全量重派生；分组层 events 引用 + roundDurations 内容双重不变时返回上次结果引用，下游 memo 不失效；零变更帧 O(n) 指针比对后直接返回上次结果。depthOf 的断链/假根判定改为可记忆化结论（链上节点共享同一 undefined 判定，一次记下）。buildTrajectory 保持纯函数契约（新增可选 hooks/finalState，输出形状不变）。轨迹视图与地图画布两处接入。10 例新契约测试（probe 计数桩钉死：首调全量、流式追加只计新增、尾部替换只计被替换位、零变更帧不计且引用稳定、前插/路径变更全量、与一次性 buildTrajectory 任意操作序列共价、toolResult 回填跨断点稳定、分组层引用稳定）。
  - EN: The trajectory/map derivation is now incrementally cached, decoupling per-frame recompute from total entry count (P1-11). The trajectory panel and turn map sit on per-frame re-referenced store output (the snapshot spreads a new array every frame, leafWalk rebuilds a new Set along snap?.entries, roundDurations is a new array every frame), so the old implementation ran the full buildTrajectoryTree over the entire history (10k+ entries after full backfill) on EVERY streaming frame — two passes plus per-entry text truncation / argument serialization, a hundreds-of-ms-per-frame hot path on long sessions. Worse, depthOf skipped memoization when the parent chain broke, degrading a single recompute to O(n²) (a head-of-window entry whose parent is outside the window is the norm). Fix: new trajectory-derive incremental derivation (turn-derive precedent) — find the common prefix by object identity, reuse prefix events and the run-state checkpoint (turn count / tool count / first-last ts / backfill index / event count), and re-derive only the incremental fragment; activePath CONTENT change (the GUI passes a new Set reference every frame — same content counts as the same path), prepend, or shrink triggers a full re-derivation; the grouping layer returns the previous result by reference when both the events reference and the roundDurations content are unchanged, so downstream memos stay valid; a zero-change frame returns the previous result after an O(n) pointer scan. depthOf's broken-chain / fake-root verdicts are now memoizable conclusions (chain nodes share one undefined verdict, recorded once). buildTrajectory keeps its pure-function contract (new optional hooks/finalState, output shape unchanged). Wired into both the trajectory view and the map canvas. 10 new contract tests (probe counter pins it: first call full, streaming append counts only new entries, tail replacement counts only the replaced position, zero-change frames count nothing and keep reference stability, prepend/path change full, output equal to one-shot buildTrajectory across arbitrary op sequences, toolResult backfill stable across checkpoints, grouping-layer reference stability).
- **会话外壳派生按拓扑版本号门控，流式帧不再全量遍历（P1-12）**：ChatView 有 11 个依赖 snap.entries 的 memo 在流式帧内全部重跑——branchChildren（O(n) 分支索引）、leafWalk（叶子链）、canvasRail（byId Map + 用户消息摘要）、activePathIds、visibleEntries、overviewEntries（还把全部条目 id 每帧 join 成签名串，O(n) 字符串分配）；常驻的消息树面板关闭时也每帧跑 buildTurnTree + filterTree 并全量渲染；画布卡片 memo 被 ChatView 传入的内联箭头函数击穿（TmNodeCard 的 props 经 TurnMapCanvas 内部 useCallback 依赖链每帧换身份）。修法分两层：MaterializedView 新增 structureRev（只在 push/prepend 时 bump，内容 upsert 不换 id/parentId 故不 bump）与 entriesRev（任何变更都 bump），snapshot() 按 entriesRev 缓存输出数组——非内容帧（turn_start/end、agent 生命周期）复用上一帧的同一数组引用；ChatView 的拓扑 memo（branchChildren/effectiveLeaf/leafWalk/canvasRail/overviewEntries 合并键）改挂 structureRev，visibleEntries 保持随 entries 走（行更新靠新条目对象传播，是流式显示的必要成本，且其依赖的 leafWalk/activePathIds 已稳定）；overviewEntries 的 O(n) 签名串换成 `structureRev:length` 键（id 集合是二者的纯函数）；画布四个回调（onJumpToEntry/onSwitchToBranch/onBranchTo/onForkAt）与 forkFromMessage/switchToNode 用 useCallback 稳定化；ContextPanel 的 messageCount/firstTs 从每帧无 memo 的 O(n) filter 改为 structureRev 门控；MessageTree 的 tree/filter 挂 open 状态，关闭时零计算零渲染。6 例新契约测试钉死版本号语义（内容 upsert 不动 structureRev、push/prepend 双 bump、非内容帧引用复用、roundDurations 引用稳定、旧快照无 structureRev 可恢复）。
  - EN: The session shell's derivations are now gated on a topology revision, so streaming frames no longer walk the full entry list (P1-12). ChatView had 11 memos depending on snap.entries that all re-ran inside a streaming frame — branchChildren (O(n) branch index), leafWalk (leaf chain), canvasRail (byId map + user-message summaries), activePathIds, visibleEntries, overviewEntries (which also joined every entry id into a signature string per frame, an O(n) allocation); the permanently-mounted message-tree panel ran buildTurnTree + filterTree with a full render every frame even while closed; and the canvas card memo was broken by inline arrows from ChatView (TmNodeCard's props changed identity per frame through TurnMapCanvas's internal useCallback dep chain). Fix, in two layers: MaterializedView gains structureRev (bumped only on push/prepend — a content upsert never changes id/parentId) and entriesRev (bumped on every mutation), and snapshot() caches its output arrays by entriesRev — non-content frames (turn_start/end, agent lifecycle) reuse the exact same array reference as the previous frame; ChatView's topology memos (branchChildren/effectiveLeaf/leafWalk/canvasRail/overviewEntries merge key) gate on structureRev, while visibleEntries stays on entries (row updates ride on the new entry objects — a necessary cost of streaming display, and its leafWalk/activePathIds inputs are now stable); overviewEntries' O(n) signature string becomes a `structureRev:length` key (the id set is a pure function of the two); the four canvas callbacks (onJumpToEntry/onSwitchToBranch/onBranchTo/onForkAt) plus forkFromMessage/switchToNode are stabilized with useCallback; ContextPanel's messageCount/firstTs move from an unmemoized O(n) filter per frame to a structureRev-gated memo; MessageTree's tree/filter gate on `open` — zero work and zero rendering while closed. 6 new contract tests pin the revision semantics (content upsert leaves structureRev alone, push/prepend bump both, non-content frames reuse references, roundDurations reference-stable, old snapshots without structureRev still restore).

### Added

- **TUI `/extensions` 仪表盘补齐子系统插件面（组件开关 + 新 kind 分组）**：此前 TUI 仪表盘有两处实质缺口——① voice/terminal/browser/computer/lsp/file 六类子系统内置单元在 ALL 视图被 `kindOrder` 白名单整组丢弃（provider 页可见、ALL 页不可见）；② 插件「包含的组件」在 TUI 完全不可见不可操作（GUI 有组件开关行）。修复：kindOrder 补六类子系统 kind（各配图标与 i18n 分组标签）；列表在插件行下渲染组件子行（缩进 + 勾选态），Space/点击开关走 daemon 同一条 `componentDenyTarget` 映射（tool → tools.disabled、stt/tts 引擎 → voice.disabledEngines 带前缀、terminal/browser/file 后端 → 各自 disabledBackends）直写隐藏设置键，下个会话工具集/引擎过滤即消费——不是展示层；检视面补「包含的组件」段（每组件状态 + 已翻译说明）。TUI 独立进程无 daemon 标注组件态，`applyBuiltinMirrorState` 扩展为同时按隐藏黑名单重解析组件状态（fail-open 与 daemon 同口径）。daemon `setComponentEnabled` 重构复用同一 helper，消除两处映射漂移面。9 例新契约测试（映射分支/TUI 组件标注/fail-open/镜像正交/子行渲染/开关回调两态）。
  - EN: The TUI `/extensions` dashboard gains the subsystem plugin surface (component toggles + new kind groups). Two real gaps existed: ① the six subsystem builtin units (voice/terminal/browser/computer/lsp/file) were dropped entirely from the ALL view by the `kindOrder` whitelist (visible on provider tabs, invisible on ALL); ② plugin "included components" were completely invisible and non-toggleable in the TUI (the GUI has component toggle rows). Fix: kindOrder gains the six subsystem kinds (each with an icon and an i18n group label); the list renders component child rows under a plugin row (indented + checkbox state), and Space/click routes through the same `componentDenyTarget` mapping the daemon uses (tool → tools.disabled, stt/tts engines → voice.disabledEngines with prefix, terminal/browser/file backends → their disabledBackends keys), writing the hidden settings keys directly so the next session's tool set / engine filtering consumes them — not a display layer. The inspector gains a "Components" section (per-component state + translated description). TUI standalone has no daemon to annotate component state, so `applyBuiltinMirrorState` now also re-resolves component state from the hidden denylists (fail-open, same rule as the daemon). The daemon's `setComponentEnabled` refactors onto the same helper, removing the two-copy drift surface. 9 new contract tests (mapping branches / TUI component annotation / fail-open / mirror orthogonality / child-row rendering / toggle callbacks in both states).

- **TUI `/settings` 插件归属键随插件停用联动隐藏（与 GUI 同一存储零漂移）**：GUI 语音设置早已按插件 state 隐藏 stt./tts. 段（voice.tsx `excludePrefixes` + 停用说明卡），TUI `/settings` 此前完全无此联动——插件停用了配置键还挂在面板里。修法：settings-defs CONDITIONS 新增四个谓词——`sttPluginEnabled` / `ttsPluginEnabled` / `browserPluginEnabled`（catch fail-open，与 daemon 同口径）/ `computerPluginEnabled`（不 fail-open，随 unsetDisabled 语义：未设置即隐藏）；schema 侧给 stt.*（含 vadEndMs/modelName/submitTrigger）、tts.*（含 localModel/localVoice/autoRead/inputMode）、speech.*（含 mode/enhanced/voice）、browser.cdpUrl/headless、computer.display/maxWidth 挂上对应 condition。**总开关本身（stt.enabled / speech.enabled / browser.enabled / lsp.enabled）一律不挂条件**——停用后仍可见可重新启用，与 GUI「说明卡 + 一键启用」等价。顺带补齐 `stt.language` 的 ui 块（此前是插件 config 里唯一没有 /settings 入口的键）。数据源是 settingsMirror 同一批设置键，双端读同一份存储，不存在漂移面。7 例新契约测试（谓词跟随/总开关豁免/language 补块/fail-open 两向/unsetDisabled 语义/number 无 options 键不进面板的负契约）。
  - EN: TUI `/settings` plugin-owned keys hide when their plugin is disabled (same storage as the GUI, zero drift). The GUI voice settings already hid stt./tts. sections by plugin state (voice.tsx `excludePrefixes` + disabled note card); the TUI `/settings` had no such linkage — keys stayed in the panel with the plugin disabled. Fix: settings-defs CONDITIONS gains four predicates — `sttPluginEnabled` / `ttsPluginEnabled` / `browserPluginEnabled` (fail-open on error, same rule as the daemon) / `computerPluginEnabled` (no fail-open, follows unsetDisabled semantics: unset means hidden); the schema attaches the matching condition to stt.* (vadEndMs/modelName/submitTrigger), tts.* (localModel/localVoice/autoRead/inputMode), speech.* (mode/enhanced/voice), browser.cdpUrl/headless, and computer.display/maxWidth. The master switches themselves (stt.enabled / speech.enabled / browser.enabled / lsp.enabled) carry NO condition — they stay visible and re-enableable after disable, equivalent to the GUI's note card + one-click enable. Also adds the missing ui block for `stt.language` (previously the only plugin config key with no /settings entry). Both ends read the same settingsMirror keys from the same storage, so there is no drift surface. 7 new contract tests (predicate follows switch / master-switch exemption / language block / fail-open both ways / unsetDisabled semantics / negative contract that number-without-options keys stay out of the panel).

- **TUI `/extensions` 检视面新增「配置：」段（插件 config 如实呈现）**：此前 TUI 能看到插件声明了哪些配置字段，但看不到当前值。修法：`applyBuiltinMirrorState` 的 updateExtension 在组件标注之外，按 `def.config` 字段声明逐键 `getRaw` 收集已落盘值，经 `coerceConfigValues` 钳制后挂 `configValues`；检视面渲染「配置：」段（label 经 t() 翻译，未翻译的 GUI i18n 键跳过，缺 label 回退字段键，空值显示 dim 的「(default)」）。编辑入口统一在 `/settings` 同一批键，检视面不重复造表单——一个事实一个家。1 例新契约测试（builtin 单元 configValues 从设置直读）。
  - EN: The TUI `/extensions` inspector gains a "Config:" section (plugin config shown truthfully). Previously the TUI could see which config fields a plugin declared but not their current values. Fix: `applyBuiltinMirrorState`'s updateExtension, in addition to component annotation, collects stored values per declared `def.config` field via `getRaw`, clamps them through `coerceConfigValues`, and attaches `configValues`; the inspector renders a "Config:" section (label through t(), untranslated GUI i18n keys skipped, missing label falls back to the field key, empty values show a dim "(default)"). Editing stays in `/settings` on the same keys — the inspector does not duplicate a form; one fact, one home. 1 new contract test (builtin unit configValues read straight from settings).

### Changed

- **兄弟轮次统一编号（撤回/重答后地图、轨迹、轮次导航条显示 2-1/2-2）**：此前回退到第 2 轮重新发问后，新的第 2 轮与旧的第 2 轮在所有概览面同名——用户无法区分激活的是哪个分支，直觉上的"2-1/2-2"式区分不存在。修法：buildTrajectory 新增轮起始兄弟索引（同父轮起始按 journal 序编号），轮标签 = 树深度 + 兄弟序（兄弟数 > 1 时 `${depth}-${sib}`，否则纯深度）；user/advisor 轮起始给后续 assistant/tool 事件带当前标签，分组层以 displayTurnLabel 透传；地图画布（节点卡/分支车道头/悬停卡）、轨迹视图（轮头/检视器）统一改为标签优先渲染。断点续派生（增量缓存）把当前标签记入运行态快照（init 恢复），增量片段与一次性全量派生标签共价。2 例新契约测试（兄弟会话标签序列/线性会话纯深度）。
  - EN: Sibling turns get unified labels (after a rollback / re-answer, the map, trajectory, and turn rail show 2-1/2-2). Previously, rolling back to turn 2 and asking again produced two turn-2s with identical names across every overview surface — users could not tell which branch was active, and the intuitive "2-1/2-2" distinction did not exist. Fix: buildTrajectory gains a turn-start sibling index (siblings under the same parent turn-start numbered in journal order); the turn label is tree depth + sibling index (`${depth}-${sib}` when there is more than one sibling, plain depth otherwise); user/advisor turn starts stamp the current label onto subsequent assistant/tool events, and the grouping layer passes it through as displayTurnLabel; the map canvas (node cards / branch lane headers / hover cards) and the trajectory view (turn headers / inspector) all render the label first. The resumable derivation (incremental cache) records the current label in the run-state checkpoint (restored from init), so incremental fragments produce labels equivalent to a one-shot full derivation. 2 new contract tests (sibling-session label sequence / linear session plain depth).

- **统一「扩展与插件」命名口径，清掉「扩展控制中心」残留**：设置导航在早前已改名「扩展与插件」，但中心自身的呈现名仍是旧名。对齐三处——GUI ExtensionsCenter 标题块（ext center title）、TUI `/extensions` 仪表盘顶栏、TUI `/extensions` 命令描述——全部改为「扩展与插件 / Extensions & plugins」；顺带移除 client-core 词典中已无组件引用的死键 "extensions control center"。口径边界不变：遗留 omp/pi 包仍称扩展（OMP Extension Packages provider 不动），自有 cordis 单元仍称插件（MusePi Plugins providerName 不动），本次只统一汇总中心的呈现名。命令名 `/extensions`、provider id 等持久化标识符不变。
  - EN: Unify the "Extensions & plugins" naming and remove the leftover "Extension Control Center" labels. The settings nav was renamed to 扩展与插件 earlier, but the center's own presentation kept the old name. Three surfaces now align — the GUI ExtensionsCenter title block (ext center title), the TUI `/extensions` dashboard top border, and the TUI `/extensions` command description — all read "Extensions & plugins" / 扩展与插件; the dead dictionary key "extensions control center" (no component references it) is removed from client-core. The naming boundary is unchanged: legacy omp/pi packages stay "extensions" (the OMP Extension Packages provider is untouched), our own cordis units stay "plugins" (the MusePi Plugins providerName is untouched) — this cut only unifies the umbrella center's display name. Persistent identifiers (`/extensions` command name, provider ids) do not change.

## [0.5.2] - 2026-10-01

### Added

- **文件子系统插件化（插件化第五刀：musepi:file 包）**：文件子系统从「散置的工具 + 索引」收编为一个内置插件单元（dsh fs 插件族 parity：tool-fs / tool-str-replace-editor / tool-fs-search）——`read`（read 工具族：文件 / pdf / sqlite / archive 选择器）/ `write`（write / edit / ast_edit 写入编辑族）/ `search`（glob / grep / ast_grep 检索族）/ `index`（工作区文件内容索引扫描）四个真实后端组件独立启停。新增 file-backend deny 通道：组件开关写隐藏键 `file.disabledBackends`（settings-schema 登记、fail-soft 读取），消费哲学与 terminal/browser 组件一致——read/write/search 被禁后对应工具族不再进入会话工具集（tools/index.ts isToolAllowed 谓词消费，禁用是显式意图、不静默留桩），index 被禁后 daemon 停止后台扫描（server.ts index.scan 门消费；已有 FTS 数据的查询面仍由 index.setEnabled 总开关治理，不越权）。无总开关语义（不发明）：file 行只读展示，禁用粒度落到后端组件。extensions.list 组件面如实下发；组件名/描述/kind 标签 i18n 键 zh/en 同步。9 例新契约测试（注册表投影/名单写读/list 下发/谓词映射/扫描门）。
  - EN: File subsystem pluginization (builtin-plugin cut five: the musepi:file package). The file subsystem moves from scattered tools + index to one builtin plugin unit (dsh fs plugin family parity: tool-fs / tool-str-replace-editor / tool-fs-search) — four real backend components toggle independently: `read` (the read tool family: files / pdf / sqlite / archive selectors) / `write` (write / edit / ast_edit) / `search` (glob / grep / ast_grep) / `index` (the workspace file-content index scan). New file-backend deny channel: component toggles write the hidden key `file.disabledBackends` (settings-schema registered, fail-soft read), consumed with the same explicit-intent philosophy as the terminal/browser components — denied read/write/search families leave the session tool set (tools/index.ts isToolAllowed predicate; disabling is explicit intent, never a silent stub), and a denied `index` stops the daemon's background scan (server.ts index.scan gate; querying existing FTS data stays governed by the index.setEnabled master switch, no overreach). No master switch (not invented): the file row is read-only display, disable granularity lands on backend components. extensions.list reports the component plane truthfully; component name/description/kind label i18n keys registered zh/en. 9 new contract tests (registry projection / denylist write+read / list projection / predicate mapping / scan gate).

- **浏览器后端组件化（插件化第四刀：musepi:browser 包）**：browser 内置单元从「整工具单组件（tool-deny）」改为三个真实后端组件——`launch`（脚本启动的 Chromium，browser.headless 控制有头/无头，工具兜底路径）/ `attach`（接管已有浏览器：CDP 端点、Relay 扩展、cmux、spawn-and-attach）/ `gui`（托管面板浏览器桥，agent 页面开进右侧面板）。新增 browser-backend deny 通道：组件开关写隐藏键 `browser.disabledBackends`（settings-schema 登记、fail-soft 读取），browser 工具解析链真实消费——设置链（relay → cdpUrl → cmux → gui → headless）回退跳过被禁环节（不是试过再失败），显式 `app.path/cdp_url/relay` 指向被禁后端给结构化 `DISABLED_BACKEND`，三后端全禁给 `NO_BACKEND`（与终端 provider 组件同哲学：禁用是显式意图，不静默回退）。extensions.list 组件面如实下发；组件名/描述 i18n 键 zh/en 同步（launch/attach/gui）；computer 行保持 tool-deny 单组件 + unsetDisabled 包形态，经注册表投影契约测试守。14 例新契约测试（通道写读/list 下发/结构化拒绝/回退语义/全禁报错）。
  - EN: Browser backend componentization (builtin-plugin cut four: the musepi:browser package). The browser builtin unit moves from a single whole-tool component (tool-deny) to three real backend components — `launch` (scripted Chromium, headed/headless via browser.headless, the tool's fallback path) / `attach` (take over an existing browser: CDP endpoint, relay extension, cmux, spawn-and-attach) / `gui` (managed in-app browser bridge; agent pages open in the right pane). New browser-backend deny channel: component toggles write the hidden key `browser.disabledBackends` (settings-schema registered, fail-soft read), and the browser tool's resolution chain consumes it for real — the settings chain (relay → cdpUrl → cmux → gui → headless) skips denied candidates (never "tried and failed"), explicit `app.path/cdp_url/relay` pointing at a denied backend yields a structured `DISABLED_BACKEND`, and all three denied yields `NO_BACKEND` (same philosophy as the terminal provider components: disabling is an explicit intent, never silently fallen back). extensions.list reports the component plane truthfully; component name/description i18n keys are registered zh/en (launch/attach/gui); the computer row keeps its tool-deny single component + unsetDisabled package shape, guarded by a registry projection contract test. 14 new contract tests (channel write/read, list projection, structured rejection, fallback semantics, all-denied error).

### Fixed

- **打开历史会话不再无条件激活（快照优先打开路径，P0-6）**：桌面打开路径此前先试 `session.subscribe`——其内部对非 live 会话无条件 `activate`（完整 AgentSession + MCP + discovery），于是每点开一个历史会话都拉起整套运行时，「历史会话只读快照、按需激活」的设计成死代码，`MAX_LIVE_SESSIONS=8` 与空闲 30 分钟回收被穿透。修法：打开路径改为 `session.resume` 首选——resume 本就「快照恒返回、仅当会话已 live 才在响应写出后附着流」（P0-7 的 catchup 语义），`session.subscribe` 退居显式流附着的兜底；配套两处 server 侧对齐——resume 的快照经 `tailSnapshot` 收敛为尾窗 + `tail` 分页契约（与 subscribe 的 initial 同形），live 时快照附带 `activeTools`/`agentsProgress` 水合（subscribe 路由 composer dock / swarm 卡首帧的等价物，`ResumeLiveHandle` 扩展两个可选结构字段）。激活语义不变：发送（session.send 激活 + 重挂发送连接）与分支操作（branchAt 一族）仍是显式写路径。3 例新契约测试（历史会话 resume 零激活零附着 + 负契约无水合键/live 会话 resume 照常附着带水合/send 按需激活并重挂发送方）。
  - EN: Opening a history session no longer activates it unconditionally (snapshot-first open path, P0-6). The desktop open path used to try `session.subscribe` first — which unconditionally `activate`s a non-live session (full AgentSession + MCP + discovery), so every opened history session spun up the whole runtime, the "history sessions are read-only snapshots, activate on demand" design became dead code, and MAX_LIVE_SESSIONS=8 / 30-minute idle reclaim were pierced. Fix: the open path now prefers `session.resume` — resume already returns the snapshot unconditionally and attaches the stream after the response is written ONLY when the session is live (the P0-7 catchup semantics); `session.subscribe` falls back to explicit-stream attach. Two server-side alignments ship with it: the resume snapshot is now wrapped in `tailSnapshot` (tail window + `tail` paging contract, same shape as subscribe's initial), and a live snapshot carries the `activeTools`/`agentsProgress` hydration (the subscribe route's composer-dock / swarm-card first-paint equivalent — `ResumeLiveHandle` gains two optional structural fields). Activation semantics are unchanged: sending (session.send activates + re-attaches the sending connection) and branch operations (the branchAt family) remain the explicit write paths. 3 new contract tests (history-session resume activates/attaches nothing + no hydration keys (negative contract) / live-session resume still attaches with hydration / send activates on demand and re-attaches the sender).

- **并发 resume 不再跨连接串线（resumeLive 登记按连接归属，P0-7）**：`session.resume` 写入、响应写出后由 `catchupIfNeeded` 读取消费的 live 订阅登记，此前是 server 上的单字段——桌面与 guest（或两个窗口）并发 resume 不同会话时后写覆盖先写：连接 A 的 catchup 读到连接 B 刚 resume 的会话，A 被 attach 到 B 的实时流（帧再被客户端 sessionId 守卫全丢，表现为会话开着却永远收不到消息），B 的 catchup 读到 null、订阅永不发生。修法：登记改为 `WeakMap<连接对象, LiveSession>` 按连接归属（连接断开即自动释放，无泄漏），SessionService 写半签名改为 `(conn, live)` 原样透传连接对象，读取侧只消费并清除本会话的登记。4 例新契约测试（交错双连接各自 attach 自己的会话/无登记 catchup 不 attach/非 live 会话不留登记/登记消费一次即失效）。
  - EN: Concurrent resumes no longer cross-wire between connections (resumeLive registration attributed per connection, P0-7). The live-subscription registration written by `session.resume` and consumed by `catchupIfNeeded` after the response is written used to be a single server field: when the desktop and a guest (or two windows) resumed different sessions concurrently, the later write clobbered the earlier one — connection A's catchup read connection B's just-resumed session and attached A to B's live stream (frames then all dropped by the client's sessionId guard, surfacing as "session open but never receiving messages"), while B's catchup read null and the subscription never happened. Fix: the registration becomes a `WeakMap<connection object, LiveSession>` keyed by connection (released automatically when the connection drops, no leak); the SessionService write-half signature becomes `(conn, live)` passing the connection object through untouched; the read side consumes and clears only its own connection's registration. 4 new contract tests (interleaved two-connection resumes each attach their own session / catchup without registration attaches nothing / non-live session leaves nothing pending / registration consumed exactly once).

- **归档/无 journal 会话恢复不再丢记录族（jsonl 投影重写为唯一规范实现）**：CLI 创建或归档、daemon 从未 journal 的会话恢复时走 `snapshotFromJsonl` 投影，旧实现只保留 `type==="message"` 记录——真实会话文件里 custom（顾问卡等）/custom_message/compaction/model_change/mode_change/title_change 等全部丢弃，恢复出的会话丢压缩分隔线与顾问卡，且轨迹与转录对比结构性失真。修法：新建规范模块 `src/daemon/jsonl-snapshot.ts`（server.ts 死副本删除，session-host.ts 本地副本同步收敛，三个事实源合一），保全全部记录族（未知未来类型前向兼容透传），独立的 256 字节 title slot 行收割进 `header.title`；全部条目经 view-key 空间重映射——message → `messageKey`（`role:ts`，同毫秒同角色碰撞加 `#n` 后缀）、非 message → `type:tsMs`，`parentId` 一律解析到**最近 message 祖先的带后缀 viewId**（非 message 父节点对分支树消费者必然悬空）；`cursor` 改为必传 journal 序水位——GUI 用 `snapshot.cursor` 种子化 M1.4 水位，旧实现传条目数会把所有实时帧当「回放」丢弃。两个调用点（adopt 回退、idle-history 回退）同改传 journal 尾序。6 例新契约测试（记录族保全/坏行 fail-soft/messageKey 形态与碰撞后缀/父链解析不悬空/cursor 语义/title 收割）。
  - EN: Archived / never-journalled session recovery no longer drops record families (the jsonl projection rewritten as the single canonical implementation). When a CLI-created or archived session this daemon never journaled was recovered, `snapshotFromJsonl` projected it into the snapshot shape — and the old implementation kept only `type==="message"` records: everything else in a real transcript (custom advisor cards, custom_message, compaction, model_change, mode_change, title_change, …) was silently discarded, so recovered sessions lost compaction dividers and advisor cards, and trajectory-vs-transcript comparisons broke by construction. Fix: new canonical module `src/daemon/jsonl-snapshot.ts` (the dead copy in server.ts deleted, the local copy in session-host.ts converged onto it — three sources of truth become one), preserving every record family (unknown future types pass through untouched for forward compat), and harvesting the standalone 256-byte title slot line into `header.title`; every entry is rekeyed into the view identity space — messages to `messageKey` (`role:ts`, same-ms same-role collisions suffixed `#n`), non-messages to `type:tsMs` — and `parentId` always resolves to the nearest MESSAGE ancestor's suffixed view id (a non-message parent necessarily dangles for branch-tree consumers). `cursor` is now a required journal-seq watermark: the GUI seeds its M1.4 watermark from `snapshot.cursor`, and the old entry-count cursor dropped every live frame as a "replay". Both call sites (adopt fallback, idle-history fallback) pass the journal tail seq. 6 new contract tests (family preservation, fail-soft on corrupt lines, messageKey shape + collision suffix, parent-chain resolution without dangles, cursor semantics, title harvest).

- **会话工作区 slug 归一化（同一目录不再分裂成两个工作区）**：同一物理目录经不同大小写/符号链接写法（如 `C:\Users\...` vs `c:\users\...`）此前会生成两个 slug 目录、在项目页显示为两个工作区。修法：slug 生成改走 `normalizePathForComparison`（realpath + win32 小写归一），canonical cwd/home/temp 三处统一；现存大小写变体目录**复用不重命名**（win32 `existsSync` 实测大小写不敏感会误命中，改经 readdir 精确→模糊两级匹配，首次出现的写法赢得目录名，旧目录继续用）；hashed 遗物哈希键保持 `resolveEquivalentPath` 原始大小写不变（17.2.5-17.2.8 遗物按原始大小写哈希，换键会搁浅旧会话）；`peekDefaultSessionDir` 与 `computeDefaultSessionDir` 共享同一变体解析，探测/创建永远一致（ghost 对账依赖此一致性）；`resolveManagedSessionRoot` basename 匹配 win32 大小写不敏感，认领旧写法目录（否则被判 unmanaged，GC/归档会漏）。2 例契约测试（win32 条件）。
  - EN: Session-workspace slug normalization (one directory no longer splits into two workspaces). The same physical directory reached via different case/symlink spellings (e.g. `C:\Users\...` vs `c:\users\...`) previously produced two slug dirs and showed as two workspaces in the projects tab. Fix: slug generation now goes through `normalizePathForComparison` (realpath + win32 lowercase normalization), unified across the canonical cwd/home/temp sites; existing case-variant dirs are REUSED, never renamed (win32 `existsSync` is case-insensitive in practice and false-matches — resolution now does readdir exact→fuzzy two-level matching, first-seen spelling wins the dir name, old dirs keep serving); hashed-legacy hash keys keep the case-preserving `resolveEquivalentPath` unchanged (17.2.5-17.2.8 artifacts hash the original case — rekeying would strand old sessions); `peekDefaultSessionDir` and `computeDefaultSessionDir` share the same variant resolution so probing and creating always agree (the ghost reconciliation depends on this); `resolveManagedSessionRoot`'s basename match is case-insensitive on win32 so it claims old-spelling dirs (otherwise they'd be judged unmanaged and GC/archive would skip them). 2 contract tests (win32-gated).
- **项目页幽灵目录自动清理（Temp\daemon-* 不再赖在列表里）**：幽灵条目真凶不在 daemon 也不在 view store（两处均已对账干净），而在 GUI——项目页列表持久化在 localStorage（`musepi-gui-projects`），从 session cwd 种子进来的测试/临时工作区（Temp\daemon-viewkey-* 等）删除后永远留在列表。修法：新增只读 RPC `fs.stat`（FileService，任何错误归一为 exists:false 绝不抛错），侧栏对每个已持久化项目路径探测磁盘存在性，消失的一并从列表/折叠态/忽略墓碑中移除。4 例 fs.stat 契约测试。
  - EN: Ghost folders in the projects tab are cleaned up automatically (Temp\daemon-* no longer lingers in the list). The ghosts lived neither in the daemon nor the view store (both reconcile clean now) but in the GUI: the projects-tab list is persisted in localStorage (`musepi-gui-projects`), and test/temp workspaces seeded from session cwds (Temp\daemon-viewkey-* etc.) stayed in the list forever after deletion. Fix: a new read-only `fs.stat` RPC (FileService — any error normalizes to exists:false and never throws), and the sidebar probes every persisted project path against disk existence, dropping vanished ones from the list, collapse state, and dismissal tombstones together. 4 fs.stat contract tests.

- **死工作区幽灵行自动对账（项目列表不再被已删除的测试/临时工作区刷屏）**：view store 物化行比 transcript 长寿——测试/临时工作区删除后 store 行仍在，`session.list` 与侧栏项目页永远列出死工作区（pi-advisor-toggle-*/daemon-forkat-* 曾刷屏）。`knownSessions` 合并时新增对账：非 live 且磁盘证据消失的行从合并结果与 view store 一并删除——① transcript 文件消失（扫描缓存内陈旧 sessionFile 路径探测）；② store-only 行（activation 物化行，持久层不记路径）的 cwd 对应 slug 产物目录整个消失（新 `peekDefaultSessionDir` 纯查询推导目录名，无迁移/建目录副作用）。负契约：磁盘文件还在的行保留；目录还在的 store-only 行保留（persist 前 lazy 阶段无法证明消失即不动）；live 会话恒保留。另：daemon 启动时一次性清扫 sessions 根下的空 slug 目录（rmdirSync 只删真空目录，有产物的目录抛错跳过）——删掉全部会话后残留的空壳工作区目录不再赖着。4 例契约测试（文件级删除/工作区级删除/两条不误删负契约）。
  - EN: Ghost dead-workspace rows are reconciled automatically (the project list no longer floods with deleted test/temp workspaces). View-store materialized rows outlive their transcripts — once a test/temp workspace was deleted, its store rows stayed behind and session.list / the sidebar project tab listed the dead workspace forever (pi-advisor-toggle-* / daemon-forkat-* once flooded the list). knownSessions now reconciles during the merge: rows that are not live AND whose disk evidence is gone are dropped from the merged result AND from the view store — ① the transcript file vanished (probed via the stale sessionFile path inside the scan cache), ② a store-only row (an activation materialized row; the persistence layer records no path) whose cwd's slug artifact dir disappeared entirely (new `peekDefaultSessionDir` computes the dir name read-only — no migration, no mkdir side effects). Negative contracts: rows whose disk file survives are kept; store-only rows whose dir survives are kept (a pre-persist lazy session cannot be proven gone, so it stays); live sessions are always kept. Plus: the daemon sweeps empty slug dirs under the sessions root once at startup (rmdirSync only removes truly empty dirs — anything with content throws and stays), so the empty shell of a fully-deleted workspace no longer lingers. 4 contract tests (file-level delete / workspace-level delete / two don't-delete negative contracts).

- **会话树子行去掉 ├─/└─ 枝线符号**：侧栏会话树此前给每个子会话行画 `├─`/`└─` 连接符，层级已靠缩进与折叠 chevron 表达，连线属冗余视觉噪音。现 treePrefix 只保留缩进空格，flattenTree 的 showConnector/isLast 字段与 SessionRow 对应 props 一并移除（消息树 lib/message-tree 的连接线语义独立，不受影响）。
  - EN: Session-tree child rows drop the ├─/└─ connector glyphs. The sidebar tree drew a connector for every child row even though hierarchy is already expressed by indentation and the fold chevron — the lines were redundant visual noise. treePrefix now renders indentation spaces only; flattenTree's showConnector/isLast fields and the SessionRow props are removed (the message tree's connector semantics in lib/message-tree are independent and unaffected).

- **孤立的顾问会话顶层行（激活过的顾问/子会话丢失 parentId）**：顾问 transcript 被点击激活后，daemon 以顾问文件头内部 id 在 view store 落物化行（parent_id = NULL）；`knownSessions` 合并磁盘扫描时 existing 分支只回填 subagent/advisor 标，**parentId 被丢弃**——`session.tree` 按 parentId 挂父节点，缺它激活过的顾问行被提为顶层孤立行（磁盘结构完好：父 jsonl + `<parentBase>/__advisor.jsonl` 都在）。修法：收编循环把父 id 推导（产物目录名 `<timestamp>_<parentId>` 后缀）移到 existing 分支之前，已有行缺 parentId 时一并回填。3 例契约测试（无 store 行对照 / 有 store 行回填 / 父行不误标）。
  - EN: Orphaned advisor sessions rendered as top-level rows (activated advisor/sub sessions lost their parentId). Clicking an advisor transcript activates it: the daemon materializes a view-store row under the advisor file's header-internal id (parent_id = NULL). `knownSessions`' merge backfilled only the subagent/advisor flags in the existing-row branch and DROPPED the parentId — session.tree attaches children by parentId, so any activated advisor row was promoted to an isolated root (the disk structure is intact: parent jsonl + `<parentBase>/__advisor.jsonl` both present). Fix: the parent-id derivation (artifact-dir name suffix `<timestamp>_<parentId>`) moves ahead of the existing-row branch, which now backfills parentId when the stored row lacks it. 3 contract tests (no-store-row control / store-row backfill / parent row not mis-tagged).

- **进入顾问/子会话再返回后消息污染所有会话（dup entry id 导致 React 残留 DOM）**：根因两层。① daemon `#doActivate` 把 advisor transcript 收编进会话视图时，message 条目按 `messageKey(role:timestamp)` 重键——同一毫秒的两条「Session update」user 消息生成**完全相同**的 id；② React 对重复 key 的列表做 reconciliation 时，dup key 的第一个 fiber 被挤出 existingChildren map 而**永远不被 unmount**，其 DOM 节点残留在 `.gui-transcript` 容器里——切回父会话后顾问的行叠在转录前面，之后点任何会话都在，重启才消失。修复：daemon 重键循环对 message 条目也加碰撞序号后缀（`#n`，与原非 message 条目同规则）；GUI Transcript 侧加 `rowKeys` 备忘——首个出现保留裸 id（prepend 分页的行身份依赖），重复 id 加 `#n` 后缀，两处渲染 map 统一取用。端到端复现脚本（隔离 daemon + playwright 真实点击）验证：返回父会话后转录行数 22→6 干净。
  - EN: Messages polluted every session after visiting the advisor/sub session and coming back (dup entry ids → orphaned React DOM). Two layers to the root cause. ① When the daemon's `#doActivate` adopts the advisor transcript into the session view, message entries are rekeyed by `messageKey(role:timestamp)` — two "Session update" user messages in the same millisecond produce the EXACT same id; ② React's reconciliation for duplicate-keyed lists drops the first fiber of a dup pair from the existingChildren map and never unmounts it, leaving its DOM nodes inside `.gui-transcript` — switching back to the parent rendered the advisor rows on top of the transcript, they stayed through every later session click, and only a restart cleared them. Fix: the daemon rekey loop applies the collision-sequence suffix (`#n`) to message entries too (same rule as the non-message branch); the GUI Transcript gains a `rowKeys` memo — first occurrence keeps the bare id (prepend-paging row identity depends on it), duplicates get a `#n` suffix, and both render maps consume it. Verified end-to-end with the reproduction script (isolated daemon + real playwright clicks): transcript rows after returning to the parent go 22→6, clean.
- **顾问 transcript 行丢失子代理/顾问标记（view store 同 id 行合并被跳过）**：`knownSessions` 合并子代理扫描结果时，`merged.has(h.id)` 直接 `continue`——污染事故期间被收编进物化视图 store 的顾问会话行（store 行无层级标记）因此永远拿不到 `subagent/advisor` 标，`session.list`/`session.tree` 把顾问行显示为普通顶层会话。修法：命中已有行时不再跳过，而是把磁盘扫描的层级标记补进已有行（subagent 置真、advisor 按文件名为 `__advisor[.<slug>].jsonl` 置真、sessionFile 空缺回填）。复现脚本验证：顾问行 `subagent:true, advisor:true`。
  - EN: Advisor transcript rows lost their subagent/advisor flags (view-store row shadowed the merge). `knownSessions` skipped merging when `merged.has(h.id)` was true — the advisor session row adopted into the materialized view store during the pollution incident (store rows carry no hierarchy flags) therefore never received the `subagent/advisor` tags, and `session.list`/`session.tree` showed the advisor row as a plain top-level session. Fix: on an existing row, instead of skipping, backfill the disk scan's hierarchy flags into it (subagent set true, advisor set true when the file is `__advisor[.<slug>].jsonl`, sessionFile backfilled when missing). Verified with the reproduction script: the advisor row reports `subagent:true, advisor:true`.
- **浮层延伸进顶栏/标题栏区域后点击被吞（Electron 拖拽区原生命中规则）**：输入框「+」添加列表、设置页配置项下拉等浮层向上展开超过 header 后，落在拖拽区内的条目点击无效、变成拖动窗口。根因：Electron 只对拖拽区的**后代**元素 honoring `-webkit-app-region: no-drag`，而这些浮层经 portal 挂在 #root 层（拖拽 header 的兄弟节点），不是后代。修法：gui-base.css 新增统一规则，全部交互浮层根（gui-menu-popup / gui-context-menu / gui-palette(-backdrop) / gui-btw-card / gui-rail-tooltip / gui-session-hover-card）显式 `no-drag` 退出拖拽命中——useFloatingMenu 全仓 80 处消费（含 GuiSelect 设置下拉、ModelSelector、AttachMenu）一处修复全覆盖。
  - EN: Clicks swallowed when a floating overlay extended into the header/titlebar area (Electron drag-region native hit-testing). The composer「+」add list, settings config dropdowns and similar menus open upward past the header, and entries landing inside the drag region did nothing — the click started a window drag instead. Root cause: Electron only honors `-webkit-app-region: no-drag` on DESCENDANTS of a drag region, while these overlays portal into #root (siblings of the drag header), not descendants. Fix: one consolidated rule in gui-base.css marks every interactive overlay root (`gui-menu-popup` / `gui-context-menu` / `gui-palette(-backdrop)` / `gui-btw-card` / `gui-rail-tooltip` / `gui-session-hover-card`) explicitly no-drag — the single fix covers all 80 `useFloatingMenu` consumers repo-wide (including the GuiSelect settings dropdown, ModelSelector, and AttachMenu).
- **侧边栏能力中心直接点击落到插件市场**：改为落到「技能」tab（发现分段）；插件市场仍经悬停菜单直达。
  - EN: Clicking the sidebar capability-center button landed on the plugins marketplace; it now lands on the "Skills" tab (discover segment). The marketplace stays reachable via the hover menu.

- **会话地图三处修缮（折叠摘要 / 位置跳变 / 导航轴漂移）**：① 折叠卡摘要从单行省略号改为与展开态一致的两行 clamp——收起时不再截断丢内容；② 节点卡补 `top/left` 过渡（与既有 height 过渡同曲线）——折叠/展开引起的布局位移平滑形变，不再瞬移（平移/缩放走 transform 不触发该过渡，无性能副作用）；③ 轮次导航轴视口指示改按节点主轴坐标分段线性插值——此前世界坐标线性映射与条目等宽排列在展开节点/分支列存在时双向漂移，视口边缘对准某轮坐标时指示窗也对准该轮条目，点击定位与指示窗共用同一几何。
  - EN: Three session-map fixes (collapsed summary / position jumps / nav-axis drift). ① The collapsed card summary switches from a single-line ellipsis to the same two-line clamp as the expanded state — collapsing no longer truncates content away. ② Node cards gain a `top/left` transition (same curve as the existing height transition) — layout shifts from folding/unfolding now morph smoothly instead of teleporting (pan/zoom go through transforms and never trigger it, so no perf cost). ③ The turn-nav viewport indicator maps through piecewise-linear interpolation over node main-axis coordinates — the previous linear world-coordinate mapping drifted from the equal-width entry layout whenever expanded nodes or branch lanes existed; a viewport edge aligned with a turn's coordinate now aligns the indicator with that turn's entry, and click navigation shares the same geometry as the indicator.
- **展开轮内工具活动时头像重复渲染**：折叠轮展开后，span 内第一条 assistant 行的前一条 entry 可能是「读取了 N 个文件」这类 custom 汇总行，紧邻判定失效导致该轮再画一个头像，一轮出现两个头像。修法：折叠 span 的头像归 header 行所有——span 内其余行（headerIdx 之后、endIdx 之前）一律不渲染头像，与 openchamber「一轮一个头像」规则一致。
  - EN: Duplicate avatar when expanding a round's tool activity. After unfolding a collapsed round, the first assistant row inside the span could follow a custom summary row (e.g. "read N files"), defeating the adjacency check and painting a second avatar for the same round. Fix: the fold span's avatar is owned by the header row — no other row inside the span (after headerIdx, up to endIdx) renders one, matching the openchamber "one avatar per round" rule.
- **分支树轨迹视图工具调用显示为省略号**：`treeTextOf` 只提取 text 块，纯工具调用行没有文本 → 90 字硬切片兜底成「…」；CSS 省略号双重截断。重写：assistant 行无文本但有 toolCall 块时返回工具名汇总（单调用 `name`，多调用 `name +N`），toolResult 无文本返回空串（保持 verdict 空判定契约），有文本时只留 DOM 400 字上限不再拼省略号。
  - EN: Tool calls in the branch-tree trajectory view rendered as an ellipsis. `treeTextOf` only extracted text blocks, so pure tool-call rows had no text and fell into the 90-char hard-slice "…" fallback, doubled by the CSS ellipsis. Rewritten: an assistant row with toolCall blocks but no text now returns the tool-name summary (single call `name`, multiple `name +N`); a text-less toolResult returns an empty string (preserving the verdict-empty contract); rows with text keep only the 400-char DOM cap without a glued ellipsis.
- **插件配置表单开关缺旋钮**：`ConfigFieldRow` 的 boolean 开关只渲染 `gui-toggle` 轨道没放 `gui-toggle-knob` 旋钮，显示为一块纯色药丸；补上旋钮，与设置页/能力中心开关同规范。
  - EN: The plugin config form's boolean switch rendered the `gui-toggle` track without the `gui-toggle-knob`, showing a plain colored pill; the knob is added, matching the settings-page and capability-center switch convention.

### Changed

- **内置插件配置与组件描述全量 i18n（中英双语）**：daemon 注册表（stt/tts/terminal/browser/computer/lsp 的配置字段 label/description/optionLabels 及组件描述）从硬编码英文改为发 i18n 键，GUI 经 tLoose 解析、缺失回显原文（与 optionLabels 同一约定）；缺失 label 的字段（browser.headless/cdpUrl、computer.display/maxWidth、lsp.lazy/shared、terminal.*）补齐。新增 `plugin cfg <key> label/desc/opt *` 与 `ext builtin <name> component <id>[ desc]` 两组键，zh/en 词表同步注册（client-core parity 测试守）。
  - EN: Built-in plugin config and component descriptions fully i18n'd (bilingual). The daemon registry (config field label/description/optionLabels for stt/tts/terminal/browser/computer/lsp, plus component descriptions) now emits i18n keys instead of hardcoded English; the GUI resolves them via tLoose with raw-string fallback (same convention as optionLabels). Fields missing labels (browser.headless/cdpUrl, computer.display/maxWidth, lsp.lazy/shared, terminal.*) gain them. Two key families are registered in both zh/en wordlists (guarded by the client-core parity tests): `plugin cfg <key> label/desc/opt *` and `ext builtin <name> component <id>[ desc]`.
- **客户端参照类文案清理**：用户可见的「同款/parity」参照类表述全部改写为自描述文案（蜂群任务卡片、桌面外壳、定时任务等，zh/en 同步）；`task-card-style` 设置 schema 的 "OMP original (Classic)" 改为 "MusePi original (Classic)"（i18n 键与源字符串此前不匹配，翻译永不命中，已同步两个 settings 词表的键）。
  - EN: Client reference-style copy cleaned up. All user-visible "same-as-X/parity" phrasing is rewritten as self-describing copy (swarm task card, desktop shell, scheduled tasks, etc., zh/en in sync); the task-card-style settings schema's "OMP original (Classic)" becomes "MusePi original (Classic)" (the i18n key and source string previously mismatched, so the translation never resolved — both settings wordlists' keys are updated to match).

- **插件组件开关实时生效（dsh parity：拨开关即所见）**：三处断点同修。① 能力中心插件清单的 fetch 上一批改成 mount-only 后丢失了重拉——补回 `extensions.changed` 订阅（payload.type 判定，照 slot-host 单例同款写法）；② 设置页语音分区改接全局 extensions registry 单例（原本地自刷、无事件订阅），插件总开关/组件开关一拨，schema 行、模型选择器、测试入口同帧跟随，不再需要重进页面；③ 引擎级黑名单落地到选择器：daemon 的 `voice.disabledEngines` 按组件 id 记账，GUI 侧新增组件 id→档位键映射（whisper→fast/balanced/turbo、sensevoice→sensevoice、parakeet→parakeet；kokoro→kokoro、melotts-zh→melotts-zh）与共享 helper `hiddenTiersFromComponents`，STT/TTS 模型选择器按隐藏档位实时过滤，全部被停用时渲染提示行（新 i18n 键 zh/en 同步）；选中档位被隐藏时不再触发自动下载。设置页与插件详情弹层同一 helper、同一存储，双入口零漂移。
  - EN: Plugin component toggles now take effect live (dsh parity: the switch IS the feedback), three breakpoints fixed together. ① The capability center's plugin-list fetch lost its refetch when a previous batch made it mount-only — the `extensions.changed` subscription is restored (payload.type check, same idiom as the slot-host singleton). ② The settings voice section now consumes the global extensions registry singleton (it previously refreshed only locally with no event subscription), so flipping a plugin master switch or a component switch updates the schema rows, model pickers, and test entries in the same frame — no page re-entry needed. ③ The engine-level denylist reaches the pickers: the daemon's `voice.disabledEngines` is accounted per component id, and the GUI gains a component-id→tier-key mapping (whisper→fast/balanced/turbo, sensevoice→sensevoice, parakeet→parakeet; kokoro→kokoro, melotts-zh→melotts-zh) plus a shared `hiddenTiersFromComponents` helper; the STT/TTS model pickers filter hidden tiers in real time, render a hint row when all engines are disabled (new i18n key, zh/en in sync), and no longer auto-download a selected tier that just got hidden. Settings page and plugin detail dialog share one helper and one storage — zero drift between the two entries.
- **能力中心内切 tab 不跳转（含扩展控制中心「能力中心」CTA）**：两个根因同修。其一，CapabilityCenterPage 的 tab 是 mount 时读一次的 `initialTab`——人在能力中心时经侧边栏悬停菜单点另一个 tab 只更新了 state、组件不重挂载，永不跳转；改为受控（`tab` + `onTabChange`，app.tsx 单一 state），外部入口（悬停菜单、omp-open-capability 事件、CTA）在页面已挂载时也能切。其二，设置页叠在能力中心之上时（openSettings 保持底层视图挂载，capabilityOpen 仍为 true）点 CTA：viewSwap 的 from===to 早退分支在关闭设置之前 return，设置层一直盖着目标视图——早退分支与常规路径统一改走 `closeSettings()`（含 150ms 液态玻璃 blur-out），关闭设置本身就是跳转。
  - EN: Switching tabs inside the capability center did nothing (including the "Capability center" CTA in the extension control center); two root causes fixed together. First, the page's tab was an `initialTab` read once at mount — clicking another tab in the sidebar hover menu while already in the center only updated state and never re-mounted, so no jump; it is now controlled (`tab` + `onTabChange`, single state in app.tsx) so external entries (hover menu, omp-open-capability event, CTA) can switch tabs while the page stays mounted. Second, when settings was open on top of the capability center (openSettings keeps the underneath view mounted, so capabilityOpen was still true), clicking the CTA hit viewSwap's from===to early return, which returned BEFORE closing settings — the settings layer kept covering the target view. Both the early-return branch and the normal path now go through `closeSettings()` (with the 150ms liquid-glass blur-out): closing settings IS the jump.
- **组件开关报「Unknown method: extensions.setComponentEnabled」**：根因是 ExtensionService 路由表登记了 `extensions.setConfig` / `extensions.setComponentEnabled`，但 server.ts 巨型 switch 漏挂对应 case，调用永远落到 default 抛错。补齐两个 case；并把门禁补上反向断言——route-coverage 测试此前只查「switch ⊆ 路由表」，新增「路由表 ⊆ switch」方向（服务认领的每条路由必须能被客户端真实调到），变异验证：删掉任一新 case 测试即红。
  - EN: Toggling a plugin component failed with "Unknown method: extensions.setComponentEnabled": the root cause was that ExtensionService's route table registered `extensions.setConfig` / `extensions.setComponentEnabled` while the server.ts mega-switch lacked the matching cases, so calls always fell through to the default throw. Both cases added; the gate gains the reverse assertion — route-coverage previously only checked "switch ⊆ route table", now also "route table ⊆ switch" (every service-claimed route must be actually callable by the client), mutation-verified: deleting either new case turns the test red.
- **i18n 重复键不再白屏（fail-safe 化，根除此前两次事故的失效模式）**：client-core 双语 barrel 的模块加载守卫从「跨域重复 key 直接 throw（一个重复文案键 → 整个 GUI 白屏，已发生两次）」改为容错检测——冲突记入 `zhCNDuplicates`/`enUSDuplicates` report 并打 `[musepi-i18n] duplicate key:` 前缀的 console.error（可检索、不崩溃），词表合并语义不变。把关前移到测试必经路径：i18n parity 断言（跨域零重复 + 域文件数/键总数防缩小守卫 + 合成碰撞用例钉死「不抛错且记录冲突双方」）进 `bun test`，CI 与包级 check 都无法绕过；参照 dsh `locale-dictionary-parity.spec.ts` 的「门禁必须长在必经路径 + 防缩小守卫」设计。本次事故键 `plugin details`（guest 域已持有）从 tools 域移除。
  - EN: Duplicate i18n keys no longer white-screen the GUI (fail-safe, rooting out the failure mode behind two past incidents): the client-core bilingual barrel's module-load guard changes from throwing on a cross-domain duplicate key (one duplicated copy key → whole GUI white screen, twice already) to tolerant detection — collisions are recorded in the `zhCNDuplicates`/`enUSDuplicates` reports with a greppable `[musepi-i18n] duplicate key:` console.error (no crash), merge semantics unchanged. The gate moves onto the mandatory test path: i18n parity assertions (zero cross-domain duplicates + domain-count/key-count anti-narrowing guards + a synthetic-collision case pinning "no throw, both domains recorded") join `bun test`, unbypassable from CI or package-level checks; design mirrors dsh's `locale-dictionary-parity.spec.ts` ("gates must live on the mandatory path + anti-narrowing guards"). This incident's key `plugin details` (already owned by the guest domain) is removed from the tools domain.

### Changed

- **插件列表整卡可点 + 全局文字选中范围治理（dsh parity 两条）**：① 插件列表条目从「仅名称行可点」改为整条卡片可点——onClick/键盘激活移到卡片容器（role=button + tabIndex + focus-visible 描边），图标/描述/徽章行随之整体可点，行内开关 stopPropagation 不受影响；② 文字选中范围按 dsh 口径治理：gui-base.css 在 html/body 层设 `user-select: none`（拖过导航/标签页/设置标签/插件卡片不再误起选区、吞点击），输入框、textarea、contenteditable、pre/code 与聊天 Markdown 正文（.tr-md）恢复可选——AI 回复与用户消息保持可复制，xterm 画布自绘选区不受影响。全部窗口（主窗/气泡/桌宠/图钉/托盘菜单）共享 gui.css 单点生效。
  - EN: Two dsh-parity interaction polish items. ① Plugin list rows go from "only the name line is clickable" to whole-card clickable — onClick/keyboard activation moves to the card container (role=button + tabIndex + focus-visible outline), so the icon/description/badge area is all one hit target, while the in-row toggle's stopPropagation is unaffected. ② Selection scope governed to the dsh convention: gui-base.css sets `user-select: none` at the html/body layer (dragging across nav, tabs, settings labels, or plugin cards no longer starts an accidental selection that swallows clicks), while inputs, textareas, contenteditable surfaces, pre/code, and the chat markdown body (.tr-md) stay selectable — AI replies and user messages remain copyable, and xterm's canvas-drawn selection is unaffected. Applies to every window (main / bubbles / pet / pin / tray menu) through the single gui.css entry.

### Added

- **顾问会话标识与只读保护**：`__advisor[.<slug>].jsonl` transcript 行在 `session.list`/`session.tree` 透出 `advisor` 标记（daemon 打标 + GUI 透传链），侧栏行尾渲染 sparkling 徽章（tooltip「顾问会话」，与子代理徽章同级）；打开顾问 transcript 时输入区整体替换为液态玻璃只读说明条（「顾问记录为只读，不能在顾问会话中发送消息」，zh/en），防止向顾问内部记录写入消息。新增 i18n 键两对（zh/en 同步）。
  - EN: Advisor session identity and read-only protection. `__advisor[.<slug>].jsonl` transcript rows expose an `advisor` flag through `session.list`/`session.tree` (daemon tagging + GUI passthrough chain); the sidebar renders a sparkling badge at row end (tooltip "Advisor session", sibling of the subagent badge); opening the advisor transcript replaces the input area with a liquid-glass read-only notice bar ("Advisor transcript is read-only — you can't send messages here", zh/en), preventing writes into the advisor's internal record. Two new i18n key pairs (zh/en in sync).
- **会话右键「在迷你对话中打开」（openchamber mini-chat parity）**：会话树任意行（含子会话）的右键菜单新增「在迷你对话中打开」——Electron 侧经 `mini-chat-open` IPC 把会话 id 深链进迷你窗（`?mini=1&session=<id>`，已开的迷你窗带新目标重载并聚焦）；mini 渲染端连接建立后一次性消费深链 id 打开目标会话（重连不会重复抢占）；非 Electron 环境（浏览器开发）回退为原地打开。托盘/顶栏原有的迷你窗入口不受影响（无参即欢迎态）。
  - EN: "Open in mini chat" in the session context menu (openchamber mini-chat parity). Any tree row (including sub-sessions) gains an "Open in mini chat" item — on Electron the `mini-chat-open` IPC deep-links the session id into the mini window (`?mini=1&session=<id>`; an already-open mini window reloads with the new target and focuses); the mini renderer consumes the deep-link id once after the socket is up (a reconnect never re-steals it); non-Electron hosts (browser dev) fall back to opening in place. The existing tray/header mini-window entries are unaffected (no arg = welcome state).
- **子会话树缩进层级修正（openchamber 文件夹层级语义）**：侧栏树此前沿用 TUI 紧凑规则——单子链保持父级缩进，一个会话只有一个子代理时子行与父行平级看不出层级；改为子级一律缩进一级（`flattenTree` 的紧凑规则保留为可选项，TUI 语义不受影响），单子代理的子 transcript 也以 └─ 连接线呈现为嵌套子行。
  - EN: Sub-session tree indent fix (openchamber folder-hierarchy semantics). The sidebar tree previously reused the TUI compact rule — single-child chains kept the parent's indent, so a session with exactly one subagent rendered the child flush with its parent. Children now always indent one level (the compact rule remains an opt-in; TUI semantics unaffected), and a single-subagent child transcript renders as a nested row with the └─ connector.
- **子会话内嵌预览（openchamber mini-chat 形态的右侧面板落地）**：agents hub「子会话」分区的每行新增悬停出现的「在面板中预览」动作（picture-in-picture 图标）——点击后该子代理的完整会话就地停靠在右侧面板上层：实时 transcript 轮询（agents.transcript 增量游标）、进度统计（tokens/上下文/费用/工具数/耗时）、kill/revive 生命周期操作与 steer 聊天输入全部可用，无需离开当前主会话；面板头部另加「在主视图打开」升级按钮（external-link 图标）一键把子会话切为主聊天面（与原行点击同等目标，openchamber 返回父级的对偶路径）。行数据补齐 daemon `session.agents` 下发的 agentId（此前被丢弃），内嵌快照由注册表行合成，覆盖已退出 live roster 的子代理——名册里没有的会话同样可预览。行结构从「button 套 button」改为 div 容器 + 两个独立按钮（合法 HTML），新增 `.gui-subsession-open/.gui-subsession-dock` 样式（悬停显现、触屏常显、focus-visible 可达）。
  - EN: Embedded sub-session preview (the openchamber mini-chat form, landed in the right panel). Each row in the agents hub "sub sessions" section gains a hover-revealed "preview in panel" action (picture-in-picture icon) — clicking docks that subagent's full conversation over the right pane: live transcript polling (agents.transcript incremental cursor), progress stats (tokens/context/cost/tool count/duration), kill/revive lifecycle actions, and the steer chat input are all available without leaving the current main session. The panel header gains an "open in main view" upgrade button (external-link icon) that switches the sub-session to the main chat surface in one click (the same target as the row click — the dual path of openchamber's back-to-parent). Rows now keep the agentId the daemon's `session.agents` sends (previously dropped); the embedded snapshot is synthesized from the registry row, covering subagents that have already left the live roster. The row structure changes from "button inside button" to a div container with two independent buttons (valid HTML); new `.gui-subsession-open/.gui-subsession-dock` styles (hover reveal, always visible on touch, focus-visible reachable).

- **终端 backend 组件化（musepi:terminal 插件的「包含的组件」面）**：terminal 内置插件新增 bun-pty / node-pty 两个 provider 组件——组件开关走新增的 `terminal-backend` deny 通道写隐藏键 `terminal.disabledBackends`（settings-schema 登记，fail-soft 读取），消费侧在 terminal-provider 解析链真实生效：auto 回退顺序剔除被禁后端（没尝试、不失败回退），显式选中（manifest/settings）被禁后端给结构化 `DISABLED_BACKEND`（用户显式意图不静默回退），全禁 = `NO_BACKEND`。`auto` 保持为解析策略配置值（terminal.provider 字段），不是后端单元，不虚构组件语义。extensions.list 组件面如实下发 enabled/canToggle；契约测试 8 例（写名单不落错键、list 下发、重启用、未声明组件结构化拒绝——含把 auto 当组件拒绝、auto 剔除计数断言、全禁 NO_BACKEND、显式 DISABLED_BACKEND）。
  - EN: Terminal backend components (the "included components" surface of the musepi:terminal plugin). The terminal builtin plugin gains bun-pty / node-pty as provider components — toggles go through the new `terminal-backend` deny channel into the hidden `terminal.disabledBackends` key (registered in settings-schema, fail-soft reads), and the consumption side takes effect in the terminal-provider resolution chain for real: auto skips disabled backends (never attempted, not fail-then-fallback), an explicit selection (manifest/settings) of a disabled backend yields a structured `DISABLED_BACKEND` (explicit user intent never silently falls back), and disabling both yields `NO_BACKEND`. `auto` stays a resolution-strategy config value (the terminal.provider field), not a backend unit — no invented component semantics. extensions.list reports the component plane truthfully (enabled/canToggle); 8 contract tests (denylist written to the right key, list plane, re-enable, structured rejection of undeclared components — including rejecting "auto" as a component, auto-skip call-count assertion, all-disabled NO_BACKEND, explicit DISABLED_BACKEND).

- **子代理会话层级交互补全（openchamber parity 三件套）**：① 侧栏子会话树可折叠——父行状态方块前渲染 chevron（有子级可点、无子级占位对齐），收起态整棵子树剔除后展平，折叠集合持久化到 localStorage（`musepi-gui-session-collapse`），搜索时强制展开避免命中行被藏；② 子会话视图左上角新增「父级」液态玻璃返回按钮——数据源为侧栏树中选中节点的父节点（fork 与子代理会话均可返回，app.tsx `activeParentSession` 一次遍历派生），与右上 Chat/Canvas 浮动条同风格顶置，点击回切父会话；③ 右侧面板 agents 页新增「子会话」分区——经 `session.agents` 注册表扫描列出子 transcript 行（状态点复用 `ag-dot` 词表 + 名称 + 相对时间，5s 轮询，fail-open 保留旧行），点击行把整个主视图切到该子会话（与侧栏子行同目标）。新增 i18n 键三个（收起/展开子会话、父级、子会话、打开子代理会话，zh/en 同步）。
  - EN: Subagent session hierarchy interactions completed (openchamber parity, three pieces). ① The sidebar sub-session tree folds: the parent row gets a chevron before its status square (clickable when it has children, a placeholder keeps alignment otherwise), the collapsed subtree is pruned before flattening, the collapsed set persists to localStorage (`musepi-gui-session-collapse`), and search force-expands so matched rows are never hidden. ② A liquid-glass "Parent" back chip sits at the transcript's top-left in a child session view — the data source is the selected node's parent in the sidebar tree (both fork and subagent sessions can return, derived in one traversal as `activeParentSession` in app.tsx), styled like the top-right Chat/Canvas floating strip, and clicking it switches back to the parent session. ③ The right panel's agents page gains a "Sub-sessions" section — it lists child transcripts via the `session.agents` registry scan (status dot reusing the `ag-dot` vocabulary + name + relative time, 5s polling, fail-open keeps stale rows), and clicking a row switches the whole main view to that sub-session (same target as the sidebar child rows). Three new i18n keys (collapse/expand sub-sessions, parent, sub-sessions, open subagent session), zh/en in sync.

- **内置设计插件包组织设计稿（dsh bundle 解剖 → MusePi 包清单）**：`docs/review/0.5.0-m2.11-builtin-plugin-package-design.md`——dsh 0.2.0-rc.2 真码核实包组织层（一 npm 包 = 一 cordis 插件四件套；base/headless/web-app/sdk-app/acp-app/sdk-minimal 六 bundle 按部署形态切层、`cordis.patch.yml` 整块覆盖 config、disabled 条件表达式、子插件 `- insert:` 嵌套、客户端同走 cordis runner），给出我方映射：musepi-base/gui/tui 三 bundle 分层、8 类内置 → 10 个插件包清单（含组件划分与收编序）、patch 行 YAML 方言、builtin-registry 退化为包清单投影，以及接续收编进度表的第三～六刀排期。
  - EN: Built-in design plugin-package organization design doc (dsh bundle anatomy → MusePi package list): `docs/review/0.5.0-m2.11-builtin-plugin-package-design.md` — dsh 0.2.0-rc.2 verified at the package-organization layer (one npm package = one cordis plugin four-piece set; six bundles base/headless/web-app/sdk-app/acp-app/sdk-minimal split by deployment form, `cordis.patch.yml` replacing the whole config, disabled condition expressions, nested `- insert:` sub-plugins, the client running the same cordis runner), then our mapping: three musepi-base/gui/tui bundle layers, the 8 builtin categories → 10 plugin packages (with component splits and adoption order), the patch-row YAML dialect, builtin-registry demoted to a projection of the package manifests, and cuts 3–6 scheduled against the existing adoption progress table.

- **子代理 transcript 收编为层级子会话行（openchamber parity，名册 stopgap 移除）**：task/vibe 子代理的 transcript（`<agentDir>/sessions/<slug>/<父文件名>/<subId>.jsonl` 两层深）此前对侧栏完全不可见，只能经名册展开看一行活动 gist。本轮收编为真实会话行：daemon `knownSessions` 同 TTL 并列扫两层 glob，子 transcript 以 header id 为会话 id、从父文件名推导 parentId 挂进 `session.tree`——GUI 侧栏自动嵌套（缩进/连接线复用既有 fork 子树绘制），行尾渲染子代理标记（与 fork 分支标记同级，tooltip「子代理会话」）；`resolveResumableSession` 全局 fallback 末段新增子代理扫描——GUI 侧栏点开子会话行经 subscribe→resume 链即可完整打开（此前永远 Unknown session）；`session.list` 透出 `subagent` 标记，`session.agents` 行补 `sessionId` 字段。昨晚的名册 stopgap UI（行内 chevron + 内联名册 + app.tsx 的拉取/轮询/穿透）整组移除——子树行出现后两者重复；名册样式（`.gui-session-agent-*`/`.gui-tree-agents*`）同步删除；`session.agents` RPC 保留（AgentsPanel 未来可用）。契约测试 5 例（两层扫描命中、畸形文件丢弃、全局 fallback 命中子会话、显式 sessionDir + 关闭 fallback 时不可达、父会话仍走一层扫描）。
  - EN: Sub-agent transcripts are consolidated as hierarchical child session rows (openchamber parity, roster stopgap removed). Task/vibe sub-agent transcripts (two levels deep at `<agentDir>/sessions/<slug>/<parentFileBase>/<subId>.jsonl`) were previously invisible to the sidebar — only an inline roster could show a one-line activity gist. They are now real session rows: the daemon's `knownSessions` scans both glob depths under one TTL, uses the transcript header id as the session id, derives parentId from the parent filename, and feeds `session.tree` — the GUI sidebar nests automatically (indent/connectors reuse the existing fork-subtree drawing), and a sub-agent badge renders at row end (sibling of the fork branch badge, tooltip "Subagent session"). `resolveResumableSession`'s global fallback gains a sub-agent scan segment, so clicking a child row in the sidebar opens it fully through the subscribe→resume chain (previously always "Unknown session"). `session.list` exposes the `subagent` flag; `session.agents` rows gain a `sessionId` field. Last night's roster stopgap UI (inline chevron + inline roster + app.tsx fetch/poll/passthrough) is removed wholesale — redundant once child rows exist; the roster styles (`.gui-session-agent-*`/`.gui-tree-agents*`) are deleted in sync; the `session.agents` RPC stays (future AgentsPanel). Five contract tests (two-level scan hit, malformed file dropped, global-fallback child hit, unreachable with explicit sessionDir + fallback closed, parent still served by the one-level scan).

- **cordis 收编第四刀：terminal-core builtin 插件单元（设计稿 §2 目标形态落地）**：终端能力的 backend 注册表收进插件 fiber——新增 `daemon/terminal-core-plugin.ts`：`createTerminalCorePlugin()`（apply provide `terminal:backends`，插件拥有的 TerminalRegistry 实例）+ `createTerminalProviderPlugin("bun-pty" | "node-pty")` 两个 provider 子单元（`inject: ["terminal:backends"]` 声明依赖——cordis 排序保证 provider 不先于 core apply，实测先挂 provider 再挂 core 时 fiber pending 至依赖就绪；apply 内 registerBackend 挂 `ctx.effect` 账本，fiber dispose = backend 反注册，禁用兜底归因（§3.③）的 NO_BACKEND 由此获得真实数据源）。server.ts 收编：删除宿主直调的 `new TerminalRegistry()` + `registerBuiltinTerminalBackends()`，改挂三个插件单元；宿主只保留 RPC 薄层 + `terminal:deps` 宿主能力（envelope seq / 连接 emit / settings——与 DaemonServer 共享编号空间是既有契约），`terminal-host-wiring` 缩为仅 provide deps。backend 实现工厂（`createBunPtyBackend`/`createNodePtyBackend`）从 terminal-provider.ts 导出、与遗留 `registerBuiltinTerminalBackends` helper 共用防漂移（helper 留给默认注册表消费者与测试）。RPC 名、provider 解析策略（manifest > settings > auto）、TerminalHandle 形状逐点不变；层隔离：同类型 provider 重复挂载 = 后挂者 fiber FAILED（DUPLICATE_BACKEND），core 与先挂者无损。契约测试 5 例（同源实例解析、双 provider 在册、dispose 反注册、DUPLICATE 层隔离、inject 依赖排序）。
  - EN: Cordis adoption, fourth cut: the terminal-core builtin plugin unit lands (design doc §2 target form). The terminal capability's backend registry moves into a plugin fiber — new `daemon/terminal-core-plugin.ts`: `createTerminalCorePlugin()` (apply provides `terminal:backends`, the plugin-owned TerminalRegistry instance) + two provider sub-units `createTerminalProviderPlugin("bun-pty" | "node-pty")` (`inject: ["terminal:backends"]` declares the dependency — cordis ordering guarantees a provider never applies before core, verified live: mounting the provider first pends the fiber until the dependency appears; apply registers the backend on the `ctx.effect` ledger, so fiber dispose unregisters it — the NO_BACKEND behind disable-fallback attribution (§3.③) gains a real data source). server.ts is consolidated: the host-direct `new TerminalRegistry()` + `registerBuiltinTerminalBackends()` calls are replaced by mounting the three plugin units; the host keeps only the RPC thin layer + the `terminal:deps` host capability (envelope seq / connection emit / settings — sharing the numbering space with DaemonServer is an existing contract), and `terminal-host-wiring` shrinks to providing deps only. The backend impl factories (`createBunPtyBackend`/`createNodePtyBackend`) are exported from terminal-provider.ts and shared with the legacy `registerBuiltinTerminalBackends` helper to prevent drift (the helper stays for default-registry consumers and tests). RPC names, the provider resolution strategy (manifest > settings > auto), and the TerminalHandle shape are unchanged point-by-point; layer isolation: mounting a duplicate provider of the same type FAILEDs the later fiber (DUPLICATE_BACKEND) without touching core or the earlier one. Five contract tests (same-instance resolution, both providers registered, dispose unregisters, DUPLICATE isolation, inject dependency ordering).

- **能力中心 tab 面板常驻挂载（持久化 + 切入动效）+ 设置侧「插件」tab 摘除**：技能/插件/市场三面板（含技能的发现/我安装子分段）常驻挂载、display 切换——切 tab 不再卸载重建，滚动位置、输入态、已拉取数据全部保留，切入面板淡入上浮 180ms（display none→block 重放 keyframes）；插件清单改为进入能力中心即预取，切到插件 tab 列表已就绪。设置侧「插件」独立 tab 同步摘除（插件列表由一级能力中心统一承载，设置「扩展与插件」控制中心内部仍含插件泳道），`plugins` 从 SectionId 退役为能力别名、存量深链落到「扩展与插件」页。
  - EN: Capability-center tab panels stay mounted (persistence + enter animation) + the settings-side "Plugins" tab is removed. The skills / plugins / marketplace panels (including the skills discover/installed sub-panes) stay mounted and switch via display — switching tabs no longer unmounts and rebuilds, so scroll positions, input state, and fetched data are all preserved, and the entering panel fades in and floats up over 180ms (display none→block replays the keyframes); the plugin list is prefetched on entry to the center so it is ready when the plugins tab opens. The settings-side standalone "Plugins" tab is removed in sync (the plugin list is unified under the first-class capability center; the settings "Extensions & plugins" control center still contains plugin lanes), `plugins` retires from SectionId to a capability alias so stale deep links land on the "Extensions & plugins" page.

- **能力中心摘遗产扩展 tab + 侧边栏 hub 改悬停菜单 + 自有 provider 品牌改名 MusePi Plugins**：① 能力中心的「扩展」tab 移除——遗产 pi/omp 扩展（.pi/.omp extension，不在 .musepi 下）归设置侧「扩展与插件」控制中心管理，能力中心不再重复承载，tab 联合收窄为「技能 | 插件 | 市场」，ExtensionsRuntimeTab 组件删除、全仓无残留引用；② 侧边栏能力中心入口从「主按钮 + chevron 子按钮」改为悬停菜单——行悬停弹出三项（技能 / 插件 / 市场）跳转对应 tab，直接点击落到技能市场，180ms 延迟关闭兼容鼠标跨 portal 迁移；③ 自有插件 provider 品牌名从「MusePi Extensions」改为「MusePi Plugins」（provider id `musepi-extensions` 保持不变，避免存量数据失效；creator persona 与 musepi-help 技能文档同步；设置侧导航项「扩展」改「扩展与插件」）。
  - EN: Capability center drops the legacy extensions tab + sidebar hub becomes a hover menu + own provider rebranded to MusePi Plugins. ① The capability center's "Extensions" tab is removed — legacy pi/omp extensions (.pi/.omp extensions, not under .musepi) are managed by the Settings "Extensions & plugins" control center instead, and the capability center no longer duplicates them; the tab union narrows to "Skills | Plugins | Marketplace", ExtensionsRuntimeTab is deleted with no dangling references repo-wide. ② The sidebar capability entry changes from "main button + chevron sub-button" to a hover menu — hovering the row pops three items (Skills / Plugins / Marketplace) jumping to the matching tab, while a direct click lands on the marketplace tab, with a 180ms delayed close tolerating mouse migration across the portal. ③ The own-plugin provider brand renames from "MusePi Extensions" to "MusePi Plugins" (the provider id `musepi-extensions` stays unchanged so existing data keeps working; the creator persona and musepi-help skill doc update in sync; the settings nav item "Extensions" becomes "Extensions & plugins").

- **cordis 收编第三刀：终端 provider 注册表化（registerBackend + provider 插件化挂点，设计稿 §2）**：新增 `daemon/terminal-registry.ts`——TerminalRegistry（owner-scoped registerBackend，dsh `registerBackend`/`DUPLICATE_BACKEND` 形状 parity）：同类型重复注册 = 结构化 `DUPLICATE_BACKEND`（不允许覆盖式静默顶替）；反注册句柄 dispose 只在该注册仍是当前持有者时移除（被顶替的旧注册 dispose 不误删新 backend）；`getBackend` 未注册 = 结构化 `NO_BACKEND`——「终端后端已停用，原因：插件被禁用」归因（回退保护③）的真实数据源。`terminal-provider.ts` 静态 PROVIDERS 表改注册表驱动：`registerBuiltinTerminalBackends`（幂等，builtin bun-pty/node-pty 注册进给定注册表）+ `getTerminalProvider(name, registry)`——显式 provider 的 open 直查注册表，backend 缺席 = NO_BACKEND（manifest 显式声明严格语义，不静默回退）；auto 只在在册 backend 间按 bun-pty → node-pty 回退、跳过缺席者，全缺席 = NO_BACKEND。provider 解析策略（manifest > settings > auto）与 RPC 名、`isTerminalProvider` 取值域（含 "auto"）、TerminalHandle 形状逐点不变。`TerminalService` 增 `opts.registry` 注入点（缺省回落共享注册表）；server.ts 构造独立注册表、注册 builtin 后端，经 `terminal-host-wiring` 插件把 `terminal:backends` / `terminal:deps` 两键 provide 进 cordis 根 Context（terminal-core 插件化时的 inject 落点，strangler-fig 薄适配层），同一实例注入服务。契约测试 9 例（DUPLICATE/NO_BACKEND 结构化、反注册交错安全、builtin 注册幂等、显式缺席不静默回退、auto 在册回退与全缺席）。模型面终端工具核对结论：BUILTIN_TOOLS 无终端工具可挪（bash-interactive 是 bash 工具的 TUI 辅助），tool-terminal 插件（spawn/send/read/list/signal）留待 terminal-core 插件化刀。
  - EN: Cordis adoption, third cut: terminal provider registry-ized (registerBackend + provider pluginization hook, design doc §2). New `daemon/terminal-registry.ts` — TerminalRegistry (owner-scoped registerBackend, dsh `registerBackend`/`DUPLICATE_BACKEND` shape parity): re-registering the same type is a structured `DUPLICATE_BACKEND` (no silent override); the unregister handle's dispose removes only while that registration is still the current holder (a superseded registration's dispose never deletes the new backend); an unregistered `getBackend` is a structured `NO_BACKEND` — the real data source for the "terminal backend stopped: plugin X disabled" attribution (fallback protection ③). `terminal-provider.ts`'s static PROVIDERS map is now registry-driven: `registerBuiltinTerminalBackends` (idempotent — registers the builtin bun-pty/node-pty into a given registry) + `getTerminalProvider(name, registry)` — an explicit provider's open queries the registry directly, a missing backend is NO_BACKEND (strict manifest semantics, no silent fallback); auto degrades only among registered backends in bun-pty → node-pty order, skipping absent ones, and is NO_BACKEND when all are absent. Provider resolution (manifest > settings > auto), RPC names, `isTerminalProvider`'s value domain (including "auto"), and the TerminalHandle shape are unchanged point-by-point. `TerminalService` gains an `opts.registry` injection point (falling back to the shared registry); server.ts constructs a dedicated registry, registers the builtin backends, provides the `terminal:backends` / `terminal:deps` keys into the cordis root Context via a `terminal-host-wiring` plugin (the inject landing point for the future terminal-core pluginization, strangler-fig thin adapter), and injects the same instance into the service. Nine contract tests (structured DUPLICATE/NO_BACKEND, unregister interleaving safety, idempotent builtin registration, explicit-missing no-silent-fallback, auto fallback among registered and all-absent). Model-facing terminal tool audit: BUILTIN_TOOLS has no terminal tools to move (bash-interactive is a TUI helper of the bash tool); the tool-terminal plugin (spawn/send/read/list/signal) waits for the terminal-core pluginization cut.

- **创造模式自举工具环自动激活（creator 预设 settings 轴 + `tools.extensionMetaTools`）+ 扩展开发技能补 cordis 运行时**：回答「创造模式工具集是否完备」的落地缺口——自举工具本就有 11 个（`extension_load/reload/status/validate/rollback` 生命周期五件套 + `ext_define/run/stop/undefine/inspect` 会话级动态插件，加常激活的 `extensions_list`；实现 `daemon/extension-lifecycle-tools.ts` / `daemon/extension-runtime-tools.ts`），但此前激活唯一入口是 `/extensions` 斜杠命令，creator 预设不自激活，且实证发现 defaultInactive custom 工具初始只挂为 xd:// 设备而非顶层工具。本轮：① 新隐藏设置键 `tools.extensionMetaTools`（settings-schema，默认 false）；② SDK 会话初始工具集组装消费该键——开启时 10 个 meta 工具从会话起即**顶层激活**（并钉住不被 xd:// 分区重新挂载），显式 `toolNames` 会话不扩列；③ creator 内置预设模板 `settings: { "tools.extensionMetaTools": true }` + persona 指向自举闭环（写文件 → 加载 → 自查 → 热修 → 回退），`BUILTIN_TEMPLATE_REVISION` 4→5，LEGACY_TEMPLATES 补 rev4 creator 字面量快照（未动过的旧 creator 预设安全升级链）；④ `musepi-extension-dev` 技能文档新增「自举工具环」「Cordis 插件运行时（组件/子 fiber/兼容性预检 + agentDir `compatibility.json` 精确版本豁免）」两节，并把「改扩展必须重启 daemon」修正为 HMR 热拔插现实（入口文件 ~500ms watcher 广播、GUI ~1s 刷新、reloadExtension 热载；子模块改动需 touch 入口；只有动发现/加载机制本身才重启）。契约测试 4 例（默认 xd:// 设备态、设置开启顶层激活且不进 xd 分区、显式 false 不激活、显式 toolNames 不扩列）。
  - EN: Creator preset auto-activates the extension bootstrap tool ring (creator preset settings axis + `tools.extensionMetaTools`) + the extension-dev skill gains the cordis runtime section. This lands the gap behind "is the creator mode toolset complete": the 11 bootstrap tools already existed (`extension_load/reload/status/validate/rollback` lifecycle quintet + `ext_define/run/stop/undefine/inspect` session-scoped dynamic plugins, plus the always-active `extensions_list`; implemented in `daemon/extension-lifecycle-tools.ts` / `daemon/extension-runtime-tools.ts`), but the only activation entry was the `/extensions` slash command, the creator preset did not self-activate, and empirically a defaultInactive custom tool initially mounts only as an xd:// device, not a top-level tool. This round: ① new hidden settings key `tools.extensionMetaTools` (settings-schema, default false); ② the SDK session-start tool-set assembly consumes it — when on, the 10 meta tools are **top-level active from session start** (pinned so the xd:// partition cannot remount them), and explicit-`toolNames` sessions are not widened; ③ the builtin creator preset template gains `settings: { "tools.extensionMetaTools": true }` + a persona pointing at the bootstrap loop (write file → load → self-check → hotfix → rollback), `BUILTIN_TEMPLATE_REVISION` bumps 4→5 with a rev4 creator literal snapshot in LEGACY_TEMPLATES (untouched old creator presets upgrade safely); ④ the `musepi-extension-dev` skill doc gains "Bootstrap tool ring" and "Cordis plugin runtime (components / child fibers / compatibility preflight + agentDir `compatibility.json` exact-version exemption)" sections, and the "extension changes require a daemon restart" claim is corrected to the HMR hot-plug reality (entry-file changes: ~500ms watcher broadcast, ~1s GUI refresh, reloadExtension hot-load; submodule edits need the entry touched; only changes to the discovery/loading mechanism itself need a restart).
