  - EN: advisor card injection wiped all earlier chat messages + session-map turn numbering/duration chaos (one root cause): the daemon's live-message `parentId` stamping whitelist omitted `custom`/`hookMessage`, leaving advisor cards parentless — the GUI's leaf walk from the tail entry mistook the card for a genuine root, the active path shrank to that one card, and the transcript filter hid every earlier message; on the map side, parent-chain depths collapsed to 1 (multiple "Turn 1" nodes), titles leaked the model-facing `<advisory>` XML, and the broken path pushed every journal entry into blank branch boxes on the right. Fixed: ① the daemon whitelist now covers custom/hookMessage (stamping extracted into a testable `stampLiveMessageParentId`; cross-role parent chains share one messageKey space); ② the MaterializedView projection preserves a custom message's `parentId` (previously dropped unconditionally); ③ ChatView's leafWalk is extracted to pure logic in `lib/leaf-walk.ts`: a parentless entry that is not the oldest loaded one is always a cut chain, never a trustworthy root (no future role leak can wipe the transcript again), with the visibility filter moving alongside; ④ map advisor titles read the clean `details.notes[].note` text (same unpacking as Transcript), and `pathTurn` falls back to the journal-order number on a broken chain; ⑤ the round-duration anchor moves from "last user message" to the current turn start (isTurnStart semantics — advisor-spawned turns no longer swallow idle gaps), record/query anchors unify on the turn-start ts (legacy assistant-anchored snapshots stay readable via fallback), `endMs` is clamped to the group's last event (a polluted duration no longer stretches the Overview time domain to 200h+), and clocks carry month-day when the span exceeds 24h.

- **能力中心技能管理对齐 WorkBuddy 交互 + 市场预览弹窗规范化**：① 技能详情的「停用」按钮此前打到 `settings.set` 的只读白名单直接报错（`read-only setting: skills.ignoredSkills`）——改为头部自动触发开关（gui-toggle），停用 ≠ 卸载：仅关闭对话内自动触发、保留手动 `/` 调用，开关带语义提示，切换后抽屉内弹 1.8s 自消退确认条；批量启停同切到 `skills.setIgnored`；② 市场卡片预览弹窗此前只有入场动效（关闭即瞬卸）且 z-index 60 不在模态带——补齐 §5u 契约：z 3000、140ms snappy 退场 + 180ms 定时卸载、Escape 捕获阶段接管 + 焦点进出还原（「+ 添加技能」对话框同款一并补）；弹窗表面从自定义玻璃换成 L3 overlay 标准配方（surface 78% + 玻璃 token + inset rim + sheen + 双层影），正文次级文字从 faint 提到 muted（玻璃底对比度修复）；预览弹窗改固定高度（`min(600px, 100vh-96px)`），RPC 内容到达不再撑变弹窗尺寸；③ 技能详情抽屉高度从撑满整屏封顶为 `min(72vh, 720px)`；④ 市场底部状态栏「已停用」计数修复——此前读取不存在的 `disabled` 字段（RPC 实际返回 `ignored`）恒为 0。
  - EN: capability-center skill management aligned to the WorkBuddy interaction + market preview dialog normalized: ① the skill detail "Disable" button previously hit the `settings.set` read-only allowlist and errored (`read-only setting: skills.ignoredSkills`) — it is now an auto-trigger switch (gui-toggle) in the header, where disabling ≠ uninstalling: it only stops auto-triggering in conversations while manual `/` invocation stays available, the switch carries a semantic hint, and toggling pops a 1.8s self-dismissing confirmation strip inside the drawer; bulk enable/disable moves to the same `skills.setIgnored` RPC; ② the market card preview dialog had only an enter animation (closing unmounted instantly) and sat at z-index 60 outside the modal band — the §5u contract is now complete: z 3000, a 140ms snappy exit with a 180ms timer-owned unmount, Escape claimed in the capture phase with focus in/out restore (the "+ Add skill" dialog gets the same); the dialog surface switches from bespoke glass to the standard L3 overlay recipe (surface 78% + glass tokens + inset rim + sheen + double shadow), and secondary body text steps up from faint to muted (contrast fix on glass); the preview dialog is now fixed-height (`min(600px, 100vh-96px)`) so the arriving RPC payload no longer resizes the frame; ③ the skill detail drawer's height is capped at `min(72vh, 720px)` instead of spanning the full window; ④ the market status bar's "disabled" count is fixed — it read a nonexistent `disabled` field (the RPC returns `ignored`) and always showed 0.

- **能力中心技能详情抽屉修复（滚动跟随 + §5u 规范化）**：抽屉此前钉在能力中心滚动容器内（absolute inset 0），列表一滚面板跟着滚走——现改为 portal 到 body 的 fixed 悬浮弹层（DialogFrame 同款，z 3000 模态带），抽屉形态不变（右侧 480px、四面内缩圆角）；动效与样式对齐 §5u：背板换标准 scrim（bg 55% + blur 6px，弃纯黑 40%）、入场 160ms 纯 opacity 淡入 + 240ms `--spring-liquid`、出场 140ms `--spring-snappy`（宿主保持挂载 180ms 定时器卸载）、表面补齐 L3 overlay 配方（强底 + inset rim + sheen + 双层影）、Escape 改 document capture 相位认领并补焦点进出还原、win32/linux 背板 blur 归零 + 底色加密走既有机制；抽屉内容（3 tab、动作行、卸载风险块）不动。
  - EN: capability-center skill drawer fixed (scroll-follow + §5u alignment): the drawer used to be pinned inside the capability-center scroll container (absolute inset 0) and scrolled away with the list — it now renders as a portal'd fixed floating layer (DialogFrame-style, z 3000 modal band) with the drawer form unchanged (right-side 480px, inset rounded corners); motion and style align to §5u: standard scrim (bg 55% + blur 6px, replacing bare black 40%), 160ms pure-opacity backdrop fade + 240ms `--spring-liquid` card enter, 140ms `--spring-snappy` exit (host stays mounted 180ms, timer-owned unmount), the L3 overlay recipe completed (strong base + inset rim + sheen + double shadow), Escape claimed on document in the capture phase with focus in/out restore, and win32/linux backdrop blur-zeroing + densified background via the existing mechanism; drawer content (3 tabs, action row, uninstall risk block) untouched.

- **设置面板死按钮清理第一批（M2.10 审计落地）**：① 聊天「组件默认展开」开关修复生效——写端存 `"1"/"0"` 而读端判 `"false"`，开关此前永不生效，现统一为 `"0"` 收起、未设置或 `"1"` 展开（存量值完全兼容）；② 插件市场卡片「打开详情」不再是 no-op——轻量详情弹层（DialogFrame compact 档）展示目录元数据（描述/版本/作者/license/标签/安装量），主页与仓库链接经系统浏览器打开；扩展中心与能力中心两处重复的市场面板实现合并为共享 `MarketplaceView`；③ 自动朗读现在透传 `tts.inputMode`（`raw`/`summarize` 之外落回 sanitize 默认，与 TTS 测试卡同一契约）；④ 用量面板 7/30 天区间选择持久化（`musepi-gui-usage-range`），重启不再回弹 7 天。
  - EN: settings-panel dead-button cleanup, batch 1 (M2.10 audit follow-through): ① the chat "expand components by default" toggle now works — the writer stored `"1"/"0"` while the reader tested `"false"`, so it never took effect; unified to `"0"` = collapsed, unset or `"1"` = expanded (fully compatible with stored values); ② the marketplace card "open detail" button is no longer a no-op — a lightweight detail dialog (DialogFrame compact spec) shows catalog metadata (description/version/author/license/tags/installs) with homepage/repository links opening in the system browser, and the two duplicated marketplace panel implementations in ExtensionsCenter and CapabilityCenterPage are merged into a shared `MarketplaceView`; ③ auto-read-aloud now forwards `tts.inputMode` (anything but `raw`/`summarize` falls back to the sanitize default, same contract as the TTS test card); ④ the usage dashboard's 7d/30d range choice persists (`musepi-gui-usage-range`) instead of snapping back to 7 days on restart.

- **死设置隐藏（schema 新增 `ui.hidden` 通道）**：`spelling.*` ×3（macOS 拼写功能本体未实现）、`update.channel`（更新器不读该 key）、`tts.bargeIn`（`DictateOptions.bargeIn` 无调用方）从所有设置界面隐藏——TUI 设置面板与桌面 GUI（`SchemaSettings` 单点过滤）都不再列出；key 本身仍可经 config.yml / `config` CLI 读写并保留 schema 默认值，功能落地后摘标即恢复展示。
  - EN: dead settings hidden via a new schema `ui.hidden` flag: `spelling.*` ×3 (the macOS spelling feature itself is unimplemented), `update.channel` (the updater never reads it), and `tts.bargeIn` (`DictateOptions.bargeIn` has no caller) no longer appear in any settings surface — the TUI settings panel and the desktop GUI (single-point filter in `SchemaSettings`) both skip them; the keys remain readable/writable via config.yml / the `config` CLI and keep their schema defaults, so un-hiding is all it takes once the features land.

- **设置面板死按钮清理第二批**：① 桌面麦克风按钮现在遵循 `stt.enabled`（TUI parity）——显式关闭时按钮渲染为禁用态且口述 hook 拒绝启动新录音（进行中的录音/转写不受影响）；schema 默认 `false` 是 TUI opt-in 语义，为避免 GUI 开箱即禁用，`settings.get` 对未配置的该 key 不再回传默认值，未碰过设置的用户麦克风保持可用；② 索引库关闭时搜索不再返回陈旧命中——FTS 数据保留（清空与否是独立产品决策），但查询在 `enabled=false` 时直接报无结果；③ 设置 → 外观 → 时间格式（12h/24h）贯通时间线概览、轨迹视图、轮次地图的 8 处时钟渲染（此前全部硬编码 24h），未设置/auto 保持 24h 现状；④ 自定义供应商表单移除重复残留的模型 API/工具支持控件块。
  - EN: settings-panel dead-button cleanup, batch 2: ① the desktop mic button now honors `stt.enabled` (TUI parity) — when explicitly disabled the button renders inert and the dictation hook refuses to start a new recording (in-flight recording/transcribing still settle normally); since the schema default `false` is TUI opt-in semantics, `settings.get` no longer echoes the default for an unconfigured key, so users who never touched the setting keep a working mic; ② with the file index switched off, searches no longer answer from stale rows — the FTS data stays (clearing it is a separate product decision), but queries report no matches while `enabled=false`; ③ the Settings → Appearance → time-format preference (12h/24h) now reaches the 8 clock renderings across TimelineOverview, TrajectoryView and TurnMapCanvas (all previously hard-coded 24h), with unset/auto keeping the historical 24h; ④ the custom-provider form drops a duplicated leftover model-API/supports-tools control block.

- **交互页重复语音测试卡移除 + 「索引新文件夹」开关接线**：① 设置 → 交互底部的「语音测试」卡（播放测试语音/测试麦克风）与设置 → 语音标签页内的测试语音输入/输出完全重复——交互页副本删除，端到端语音链路测试统一归口语音标签页；② 设置 → 数据与统计 → 索引库的「索引新文件夹」开关此前写了 localStorage 但全仓无读取（死开关）——现接线为门控后台轮询里的自动 `index.scan`：关闭后切换/打开新文件夹不再自动建索引，手动打开「索引仓库」开关仍会立即扫描一次。
  - EN: duplicate speech-test card removed from Interaction settings + "Index new folders" toggle wired: ① the "Speech test" card at the bottom of Settings → Interaction (play test phrase / test microphone) fully duplicated the test-voice-input/output controls inside the Settings → Voice tab — the Interaction copy is removed and end-to-end speech-stack testing lives solely in the Voice tab; ② the "Index new folders" toggle under Settings → Data & Stats → Index Library wrote localStorage with no reader anywhere (a dead switch) — it now gates the automatic `index.scan` in the background polling: with it off, switching to or opening a new folder no longer auto-indexes it, while manually enabling the "Index repositories" switch still triggers an immediate scan.

- **设置默认值漂移与登出守卫修复（M2.10 审计收尾）**：① 打字机效果开关未设置时，设置页显示 ink 而聊天实际渲染 typewriter（同一存储键两处默认回退不一致）——设置页回退对齐为 typewriter，显示与行为一致；② 设置 → 模型与供应商的「登出」在无会话打开时静默无效（守卫要求 sessionId，但 `providers.logout` RPC 本就不需要它，单凭据删除路径也无此守卫）——去掉多余守卫，无会话也能正常管理凭据。
  - EN: settings default-value drift and logout guard fixes (M2.10 audit wrap-up): ① with the typing-effect preference unset, the settings page displayed ink while chat actually rendered typewriter (two divergent fallbacks over the same storage key) — the settings fallback is aligned to typewriter so display matches behavior; ② "Log out" under Settings → Models & Providers silently did nothing when no session was open (a guard demanded sessionId, which the `providers.logout` RPC never used, and the per-credential delete path had no such guard) — the spurious guard is removed, credentials can be managed without an open session.

- **定时任务历史完成提示启动重放**：应用启动后首次 `cron.list` 轮询把历史已有的终态 run 全部当"刚完成"弹 toast（最多 20 条连环弹窗）——首次轮询改为只建基线：终态 run 只播种已通知集合不弹窗，运行中的 run 不播种（转终态时仍正常通知）。
  - EN: cron completion toasts replayed at startup: the first `cron.list` poll treated every historical terminal run as "just finished" and popped up to 20 toasts in a row — the first poll now only establishes a baseline: terminal runs seed the already-notified set without toasting, and running runs are not seeded so their transition to a terminal state still notifies.

- **桌宠拖动逐帧胀大**：拖动桌宠时 `setPetPosition` 每帧读取窗口 live bounds 并连尺寸一并回写——读/写坐标空间不一致导致窗口尺寸逐帧棘轮式胀大；现在位置写入锁死固定创作尺寸（320×290），拖动只移位置不动尺寸。
  - EN: the desktop pet grew frame by frame while being dragged: `setPetPosition` read the window's live bounds and wrote the size back on every frame — a read/write coordinate-space mismatch ratcheted the window larger each frame; position writes now pin the fixed authoring size (320×290) so dragging only moves the window.

- **能力中心技能市场六处实机修复（安装态/预览/搜索/抽屉/图标）**：① 市场卡片装完仍显示「+」——已安装判定此前只按市场条目的显示名匹配 `skills.list`，而落盘名来自 SKILL.md frontmatter（市场卡「编程专家」装完叫 `dev-expert`），现改为 name/slug 双键判定，装完卡片就地翻绿✓，角标与预览弹窗安装按钮同步拦截重复安装；② 重复点添加弹 `already exists` 裸错误条——daemon 的 `conflict` 失败 kind 在 GUI 侧归为语义态：提示「此前已安装」并回读已安装列表，不再当错误吓人；③ 市场预览弹窗有时只有文件清单没有说明——SkillHub 详情 RPC 现带回描述并在弹窗渲染（此前只有版本/文件/安全审计）；实测 v1 详情报文的说明嵌在 `skill.summary`/`skill.summary_zh` 里而非顶层 `description` 字段，第一版修读错了层级（对所有技能都取不到），已改按真实报文形状归一化；弹窗描述粘住：detail 描述优先、回落搜索条目自带描述、同文不重复渲染，加载完成绝不比加载中信息更少；④ 技能详情抽屉此前浮在视口右上角（portal 化时锚错），按设计稿改锚定右下角；⑤ 中文搜不到技能（如「编程专家」）——远端搜索只索引 id/英文，关键词零命中时自动回退「拉一页目录 + 本地按名称/中文描述/作者过滤」；⑥ 「我安装的」卡片图标变灰色首字母块——发现页拉目录时按 name/slug 记录市场 iconUrl，已安装列表的图标优先沿用市场图，无图才回落首字母色块。
  - EN: six real-machine fixes for the capability-center skill market (install state / preview / search / drawer / icon): ① market cards kept showing "+" after install — the installed check only matched the market entry's display name against skills.list, while the on-disk name comes from SKILL.md frontmatter (card "编程专家" installed as `dev-expert`); it now matches on name OR slug, so the card flips to the green check immediately, and both the badge and the preview dialog's install button intercept repeat installs; ② repeat clicks surfaced a raw `already exists` error strip — the daemon's `conflict` failure kind is now treated as a semantic state on the GUI side ("already installed" notice + installed-list refresh) instead of a scary error; ③ the market preview dialog sometimes showed only the file list with no description — the SkillHub detail RPC now returns a description and the dialog renders it (previously only version/files/security audit); the live v1 payload nests the text under `skill.summary`/`skill.summary_zh` rather than a top-level `description` field — the first fix read the wrong level (so nothing was fetched for any skill) and now normalizes the real payload shape; the dialog description is sticky: the detail description wins, falling back to the search row's own description, never rendering the same text twice, so a loaded dialog never shows less information than the loading one did; ④ the skill detail drawer floated in the viewport's top-right corner (wrong anchor from the portal change) — it now anchors to the bottom-right per the design spec; ⑤ Chinese keywords (e.g. 「编程专家」) found nothing — remote search only indexes ids/English, so on zero hits the query falls back to pulling one browse page and filtering locally by name/Chinese description/author; ⑥ installed cards showed a gray initial-block icon — the discover pane now records the market iconUrl by name/slug, and installed cards reuse the market icon before falling back to the initial tile.

- **媒体生成视频提供商接线（创作面板视频 tab 不再空缺）**：`media.providers` RPC 此前把全部内置提供商硬编码为 `kind: "image"`——`agnes_video_gen` 工具早已可用（复用 agnes/agnes-global 凭据，模型 `agnes-video-v2.0`），但创作面板媒体→视频 tab 与设置→媒体生成的视频分组永远为空。内置列表现补上 Agnes / Agnes (Global) 两条 video 条目（凭据状态与图像侧同一存储），视频 tab 与设置页分组自动出现。
  - EN: video providers wired into media generation (the creation panel's video tab is no longer empty): the `media.providers` RPC hardcoded every builtin as `kind: "image"` — the `agnes_video_gen` tool has long been available (reusing agnes/agnes-global credentials, model `agnes-video-v2.0`), yet the creation panel's media→video tab and the settings video group stayed empty forever. The builtin list now emits Agnes / Agnes (Global) video entries (credential state from the same storage as the image side), so the video tab and the settings group populate automatically.

- **/btw 侧问浮卡飘到消息流中部盖住会话**：浮卡此前以 `position: fixed` 内联渲染在 ChatView 面板树内——面板链路上的 transform/filter 祖先（场景进场动画；被遮挡/节流的窗口会把动画冻结在其中）会把 fixed 参照盒从视口劫持到该祖先，卡片于是按错误参照盒排版、浮在消息区中部。现按 SessionHoverCard 同款契约 portal 到 `document.body`，并补视口高度上限（超出内部滚动）：任何窗口状态下都锚定输入框上方右下，不再遮盖消息流。
  - EN: the /btw side-question floating card drifted into the middle of the message stream and covered the conversation: it rendered inline in the ChatView pane tree with `position: fixed` — a transform/filter ancestor on the pane chain (scene enter animations; occluded/throttled windows freeze mid-animation) hijacks the fixed containing block away from the viewport, so the card laid itself out against the wrong box. It now portals to `document.body` per the SessionHoverCard contract, with a viewport max-height cap (overflow scrolls internally): in every window state it stays anchored to the bottom-right above the composer and never covers the message stream.

- **会话地图展开卡高度失控 + 轮内轨迹不可点击**：① 展开轮卡的布局高度按事件数线性增长（24px × N），与 CSS 泳道可视上限 240px 不同步——事件多的轮（如实测 Turn 6）展开后被撑到数千 px 高而内容只有顶部一截；布局侧封顶 `TURN_LANE_MAX_H`（超出部分泳道内滚动），节点卡高度改走 `--gui-motion-height` 平滑过渡 + `overflow: hidden` 裁剪，折叠 ⇄ 展开不再跳变。② 展开卡的轮内事件行此前只读——现带 entryId 的行可点击，经既有 `onJumpToEntry` 通道切回对话视图并定位到该事件对应消息（与卡面双击同通道、事件级粒度，点击不冒泡触发折叠），hover 有 accent 高亮。
  - EN: session-map expanded card height blowout + in-turn trajectory rows now clickable: ① the expanded turn card's layout height grew linearly with event count (24px × N) while the CSS lane caps at 240px — event-heavy turns (the real Turn 6 case) ballooned to thousands of px with content only at the top; the layout side now clamps at `TURN_LANE_MAX_H` (the lane scrolls internally), and the node card height animates over `--gui-motion-height` with `overflow: hidden` clipping, so collapse ⇄ expand no longer jumps. ② event rows inside an expanded card were read-only — rows carrying an entryId are now clickable and jump through the existing `onJumpToEntry` channel back to the conversation at that event's message (same channel as the card double-click, event-level granularity, clicks don't bubble into a fold toggle), with an accent hover highlight.

- **终端坞右缘与右侧 rail 未对齐 + 上边距双倍 gutter**：dock 基础规则带 `width: 100%`，叠加左右各 8px gutter margin 后总宽溢出父容器 16px、右缘被裁到贴窗缘（rail 右缘 = 窗缘 − gutter）——删除该宽度声明，由 flex stretch 对齐到与 rail 同一右缘。上边距此前是三处叠加（chat 卡片底 margin + chat surface 内联 flex gap + dock 自身 margin-top）导致 16px；间距收口为「一处事实一个家」（每张卡自己的 margin）：surface 去 gap、dock 改为 `margin: 0 gutter gutter`、状态条接管自身底距，chat 卡底 → dock 顶归一为 8px，启用状态条时视觉与此前逐像素一致。
  - EN: terminal dock's right edge misaligned with the right rail + doubled top margin: the dock's base rule carried `width: 100%`, which with 8px gutter margins on both sides overflowed the parent by 16px and clipped the right edge flush against the window edge (the rail's right edge sits at window edge − gutter) — the width declaration is gone and flex stretch now aligns the dock to the same right edge as the rail. The top margin was triple-stacked (the chat card's bottom margin + the chat surface's inline flex gap + the dock's own margin-top) for 16px; spacing is now single-sourced (each card owns its own margin): the surface loses its gap, the dock becomes `margin: 0 gutter gutter`, and the status bar owns its own bottom distance — chat card bottom → dock top is one 8px gutter, and with the status bar enabled the visuals match the previous layout pixel for pixel.

- **终端坞不跟随会话 + 未开过的会话/空态意外创建 pty**：dock 开关此前是全局布尔——A 会话开了终端，切到 B 会话或空态新建页坞仍保持打开；且 TerminalPanel 常挂载，任何会话视图（含 mini 窗口启动）一渲染就 `terminal.open` 建 pty。现开关按会话记忆（内存态，重启归零）：切到从未开过终端的会话/空态时坞保持关闭，切回开过的会话恢复；TerminalPanel 改为懒挂载——仅当前会话首次开坞后才挂载（此后关坞不杀 pty），从未开过坞的会话视图永不 spawn 终端实例，mini 窗口不再启动即建 pty。
  - EN: the terminal dock didn't follow the session + sessions that never opened it (and the empty state) spawned pties unprompted: the dock toggle was one global boolean — open it in session A and it stayed open after switching to session B or the empty new-task view; and TerminalPanel was always mounted, so any session view (including mini-window startup) fired `terminal.open` on render. The toggle is now remembered per session (in-memory, reset on relaunch): switching to a session that never opened the dock — or to the empty state — keeps it closed, and switching back to one that did restores it; TerminalPanel mounts lazily, only after the current session's first dock open (closing the dock afterwards never kills its pties), so views that never opened the dock never spawn a terminal instance, and the mini window no longer creates a pty at startup.

## [0.4.40] - 2026-09-26

### Added

- **技能卡片点击预览（信息先览，再决定安装）**：能力中心「发现」屏的卡片现在整卡可点，弹出液态玻璃预览弹窗——两个来源各取所长：skills.sh 由 daemon 从发布者仓库直读 `SKILL.md` 正文（其搜索接口不带描述，正文预览补上"看得到内容"的缺口）；SkillHub 走详情接口展示最新版本、文件清单与安全审计。底部安装键与卡片角标同一条来源感知路由。新增 `skills.marketplace.preview` RPC。
  - EN: skill cards are now clickable with a look-before-you-install preview: the whole card opens a liquid-glass dialog where each source plays to its strength — skills.sh previews stream the publisher repo's `SKILL.md` straight from the daemon (its search API carries no description, and the body preview closes that gap); SkillHub shows latest version, file manifest, and security audit via the detail endpoint. The footer install button rides the same source-aware route as the card badge. New `skills.marketplace.preview` RPC.

### Fixed

- **测试/冒烟会话隔离（生产会话列表不再被"未命名会话"刷屏）**：daemon 套件（branch-at/fork-at/view-key/ws-transport）与 advisor-toggle 此前用 temp cwd 起 daemon/会话，但默认会话根仍是生产 `~/.musepi/agent/sessions/<temp-slug>/`——temp cwd 只改 slug 不改根，Windows 上 daemon 句柄又让清理静默失败，生产目录曾堆积 160+ 个测试会话。这些套件现统一经 `test/helpers/isolate-agent-dir.ts` 把 agent 目录重定向进临时树（退出还原、句柄残留由 OS 清理）；手动冒烟走新脚本 `bun scripts/dev-smoke.ts -- <命令>`（注入 `PI_CODING_AGENT_DIR`/`MUSEPI_DAEMON_DIR` 隔离，自动复制 auth.json，退出即清理）。AGENTS.md 新增隔离铁律条目。
  - EN: test/smoke session isolation (the production session list no longer floods with "未命名会话"): the daemon suites (branch-at/fork-at/view-key/ws-transport) and advisor-toggle used to start daemons/sessions with a temp cwd, but the default session root stayed the production `~/.musepi/agent/sessions/<temp-slug>/` — a temp cwd only changes the slug, not the root, and daemon handles on Windows made cleanup silently fail, piling 160+ test sessions into the production store. These suites now redirect the agent dir into a temp tree via `test/helpers/isolate-agent-dir.ts` (restored on exit; handle residue left to the OS cleaner); manual smoke runs go through the new `bun scripts/dev-smoke.ts -- <cmd>` wrapper (injects `PI_CODING_AGENT_DIR`/`MUSEPI_DAEMON_DIR`, copies auth.json, cleans up on exit). AGENTS.md gains the isolation hard rule.
- **撤回/编辑重发后轮次编号与地图主线按活跃叶路径重算**：此前轮号 = journal 追加序——撤回第 2 轮再发问,新轮显示为"第 5/6 轮";地图主线用 first-child 启发式,branchAt 后旧分支常被当成主线、新主线轮反被挤进分支列,主线连线还因 SVG objectBoundingBox 渐变在垂直 `<line>` 上退化（零宽包围盒）而整段消失。现在 `buildTrajectory` 接受活跃叶路径（GUI 由 leafWalk/pinnedPathIds 提供）：在路径上 = 主线、离路径 = 分支列；轮号 = 树深度（父链上的轮起始数），新主线轮重新编号为第 3/4 轮，废弃分支保留各自深度编号并整体入分支泳道；统计轮数只数主线轮。垂直/横向主线渐变改 `userSpaceOnUse` 坐标，主线金线恢复渲染。
  - EN: turn numbering and the map main lane now recompute from the active leaf path after revert/edit-resend: turn numbers used to be the journal append order — rewind to turn 2 and ask again, the new turns showed as "Turn 5/6"; the map main lane used a first-child heuristic that kept the abandoned branch as main and pushed the new main turns into a branch lane after a branchAt, and the main connecting lines had vanished entirely because the SVG objectBoundingBox gradient degenerates on a vertical `<line>` (zero-width bbox). `buildTrajectory` now takes the active leaf path (leafWalk/pinnedPathIds from the GUI): on-path = main lane, off-path = branch lane; the turn number is the tree depth (turn-starts on the parent chain), so new main turns renumber as Turns 3/4 while abandoned branches keep their depth numbers in swimlanes; the turn count only includes main-lane turns. Main-line gradients switched to `userSpaceOnUse` coordinates, restoring the gold main connectors in both orientations.
- **系统提示词时间锚点强化**：问"今天天气"曾搜出一年前的结果——日期行深埋长提示词里，弱模型自行编造年月。日期行现带星期与显式锚点指令（"Today: YYYY-MM-DD (Weekday)… use it and never guess the year/month"），`{{date}}` 值格式不变。
  - EN: system-prompt time anchor hardened: asking "what's the weather today" once searched a year-old result — the date line sat buried in a long prompt and weak models invented the year/month. The date line now carries the weekday plus an explicit anchor directive ("Today: YYYY-MM-DD (Weekday)… use it and never guess the year/month"); the `{{date}}` value format is unchanged.
- **技能市场安装链路全面修复**：SkillHub 技能的"添加"此前把目录主页（`api.skillhub.cn/...`）当 git URL 直接 clone，必然 403——现在 daemon 新增 `skills.marketplace.install` 来源感知路由：SkillHub 走官方 `api/v1/download`（302 → 对象存储）zip 直装（fflate 解压 + zip-slip 防护 + 与 git 安装同一套 SKILL.md 定位/命名约定）；skills.sh 以 `repo + slug`（skill id 即仓库内子目录）做子目录 git 安装，修掉 monorepo（如 `anthropics/skills`）根目录多 SKILL.md 必中的歧义报错。卡片内容也有料了：skills.sh 卡片显示发布者仓库，筛选为 skills.sh 且无关键词时给出"输入关键词即可检索"的引导（不再是误导性的"没有匹配的技能"）；「推荐套件」行的来源 pill 修正为真正的 全部来源 / SkillHub / skills.sh 三选一（此前"SkillHub 套件"pill 实际选中的是"全部来源"）。
  - EN: skill-marketplace install fully rewired: SkillHub's "add" used to feed the catalog homepage (`api.skillhub.cn/...`) straight to git clone, which always 403'd — the daemon now has a source-aware `skills.marketplace.install` route: SkillHub installs via the official `api/v1/download` (302 → object storage) zip path (fflate extraction, zip-slip guard, same SKILL.md locate/name contract as the git installer); skills.sh installs with `repo + slug` (the skill id IS the repo-relative folder) as a subdirectory git clone, fixing the guaranteed ambiguous-SKILL.md error on monorepos like `anthropics/skills`. Cards carry content now too: skills.sh cards show the publisher repo, the skills.sh filter without a keyword says "type a keyword to search its catalog" instead of the misleading "no matching skills", and the 推荐套件 row's source pills are a real all / SkillHub / skills.sh three-way (the "SkillHub 套件" pill had actually selected "all sources").

## [0.4.39] - 2026-09-26

### Added

- **语音输入首次使用引导**：点击麦克风时先经引导闸口查询 `stt.modelStatus`（新增 `defaultKey`/`defaultCached` 字段，由 daemon 解析设置里的默认识别档）——模型已在本地则直接进入听写；未下载则弹出液态玻璃引导卡（说明模型名称与体积、下载时长预期，稍后/前往安装两键），下载进度走既有的全局 `stt.download*` 事件流实时呈现，完成后自动开始本次听写，失败可重试；首次点击不再把 GB 级下载塞在 `stt.transcribe` RPC 里裸超时。桌面端为液态玻璃弹窗（`voice-setup.tsx` 闸口）；client-core（guest 网页/Android 壳共用）为同流程的引导卡（`Composer.tsx` 的 `setup` 态 + `VoiceSetupCard`，含设备端识别兜底入口），两端共用 wire 契约与全局下载事件流。
  - EN: voice input first-use guide: clicking the mic now passes through a setup gate that queries `stt.modelStatus` (new `defaultKey`/`defaultCached` fields — the daemon resolves the default recognition tier from settings) — cached models go straight into dictation; a missing one opens a liquid-glass guide card (model name and size, download-time expectation, Later / Install buttons), progress renders live over the existing global `stt.download*` event stream, the interrupted dictation auto-starts when the fetch completes, and failures offer retry; a first click no longer buries a GB-scale download inside a bare `stt.transcribe` RPC timeout. Desktop client first; the guest-client counterpart is follow-up (the wire contract is already shared).

- **会话分享附件超限校验**：分享前统计快照中的附件载荷（fileMention 文件内容 + 内联图片，含子会话），超过 5 MB 时以结构化 `ShareTooLargeError` 拒绝并给出可读原因，调用方可凭 `canRetryWithStrippedAttachments` 提供「剥离附件后继续」重试；`sealToFit` 降级链第一步改为显式剥离附件并在结果中回报 `strippedAttachments` 计数，不再静默截断。剥离严格发生在脱敏之后，脱敏预扫仍覆盖完整附件字节。
  - EN: share attachment budget validation: before publishing, the snapshot's attachment payloads (fileMention file contents + inline images, including sub-sessions) are tallied; beyond 5 MB the share is rejected with a structured `ShareTooLargeError` carrying a readable reason, and callers can offer a "strip attachments and continue" retry via `canRetryWithStrippedAttachments`; the first step of the `sealToFit` degradation chain is now an explicit attachment strip that reports a `strippedAttachments` count instead of silently truncating. Stripping runs strictly after redaction, so the redaction pre-scan still sees the full attachment bytes.
- **扩展中心内置可见性补全**：内置扩展登记补齐到五类——bundled skills、magic keywords、modes 主题包、tool-render 卡片包与原先的 task-card/shell 一并纳入 `BUILTIN_EXTENSIONS`；TUI 与 GUI 的 provider tab 不再跳过 native（TUI 单独产出只读 MusePi tab、GUI 原生项改为一 provider 一节点），扩展中心统一「内置」徽章叙事，只读项（主题包/渲染器包）不渲染开关。
  - EN: extension center builtin visibility completed: builtin registration now covers five categories — bundled skills, magic keywords, modes theme packs, and tool-render card packs joined the original task-card/shell entries in `BUILTIN_EXTENSIONS`; TUI and GUI provider tabs no longer skip native (TUI emits a read-only MusePi tab, GUI native items become one node per provider), and the extension center carries a uniform "builtin" badge with read-only items (theme packs/renderer packs) rendering no toggle.

### Changed

- **设置页「模拟会话视图」预览体系（外观预览更新到当前实际 + 语音测试改同款预览）**：抽出共享组件 MockConversationPreview（状态条/用户气泡/assistant 行（Markdown 渲染+tr-actions 插槽）/活动折叠行/composer 插槽，外观开关实时联动）。外观设置：预览反映当前真实消息样式；选项分组重排——界面设置 → 效果（动效/状态条/头像）→ 效果预览 → 图片（内嵌图片独立成组）→ 本地化 → 代码设置/预览 → 终端行为（TUI 专属项归组）→ ChatSection。语音设置：测试语音输入/输出不再用孤立模拟卡——改为预览 composer 上真实麦克风按钮听写（电平条状态机原样迁移，多轮听写累积为用户气泡）、预览 assistant 示例消息（中英混合 markdown）上点朗读按钮播放（复用 speak 通道）；删除 MockUserMessage/TtsTestCard 旧实现。
  - EN: settings pages gain a shared "mock conversation view" preview system (the appearance preview is brought up to date and voice testing switches to the same preview): a shared MockConversationPreview component is extracted (status bar / user bubble / assistant row (Markdown rendering + tr-actions slot) / activity fold row / composer slot, live-linked to the appearance toggles). Appearance settings: the preview reflects the current real message styling; option groups are reordered — Interface → Effects (motion / status bar / avatars) → Preview → Images (inline images as its own group) → Localization → Code settings/preview → Terminal behavior (TUI-only items grouped) → ChatSection. Voice settings: test voice input/output drop the isolated mock cards — dictation runs from a real mic button on the preview composer (the level-bar state machine migrated as-is, multi-turn dictation accumulates as user bubbles) and the preview assistant sample (zh/en mixed markdown) plays via a speak button on the message (reusing the speak channel); the old MockUserMessage/TtsTestCard implementations are removed.
- **语音设置重构：模型选择收敛为单选项卡片 + 输入设备列表液态玻璃化 + 测试语音 UX 补完**：「语音识别模型」从四张横排大卡片（各带下载按钮）收敛为一张选项卡——每行 radio（选中即写 `stt.modelName`，选中未缓存档自动触发下载）+ 名称 + 语言/大小徽章 + 人性化场景副文案（「极速、英文为主」「均衡默认、多语言」「中文优化、中英混合」…）+ 右侧下载/就绪状态按钮（参考 dsh PreparationCard 范式、液态玻璃规范材质，零新 token）；测试语音输入的输入设备下拉从原生 select 换成全局液态玻璃浮层菜单原语（menuitemradio + 当前项 ✓）；测试语音输入/输出补完状态机（idle→录音中（秒数+实时电平条）→转写中→结果/错误反馈），开始失败不再卡死按钮，错误走已译友好文案。
  - EN: voice settings reshaped: model selection consolidates from four wide cards (each with its own download button) into a single option card — each row carries a radio (selecting writes `stt.modelName` immediately; selecting an uncached tier auto-starts the download) + name + language/size badges + human scenario copy ("fast, English-first", "balanced default, multilingual", "Chinese-optimized, zh/en mixed", …) + a download/ready status button on the right (dsh PreparationCard pattern, liquid-glass tokens, zero new tokens); the mic-device dropdown in test voice input swaps the native select for the global liquid-glass floating-menu primitive (menuitemradio + ✓ on the current item); test voice input/output gain a completed state machine (idle → recording (seconds + live level bar) → transcribing → result/error feedback), a failed start no longer wedges the button, and errors use the already-translated friendly copy.
- **会话地图收敛为单一轮级视图**：消息级画布（SessionTreeCanvas，近万条消息各一张卡的调试视图）下线，「地图」tab 永远进入轮级地图；轮卡两态折叠——默认紧凑单行（轮次徽章 + 摘要 + 事件计数 + 用时），单击展开泳道，顶栏可全部展开/折叠；泳道明细对齐 TUI /trace——工具行显示参数摘要，assistant 行显示 ↑/↓ tokens、耗时与 ttft；新增横向/纵向布局切换（选择持久化到本地）；右键菜单语义统一为「跳转（纯导航）/ 切换到此分支（移动 leaf）/ 重答 / 分叉」，重答/分叉/切换分支全部接入运行中确认保护。
  - EN: session map consolidated into a single turn-level view: the message-level canvas (SessionTreeCanvas — one card per message across ~10k messages) is retired and the Map tab always opens the turn map; turn cards are two-state — compact single line by default (turn badge, summary, event count, duration), click to expand the swimlanes, expand/collapse-all in the header; lane detail reaches TUI /trace parity — tool rows show an argument summary, assistant rows show ↑/↓ tokens, duration, and ttft; a persisted horizontal/vertical layout toggle is added; the context menu is unified as Jump (pure navigation) / Switch to this branch (moves the leaf) / Retry / Fork, and retry/fork/switch all go through the running-confirmation guard.
- **设置界面复用主界面骨架**：打开设置不再是覆盖聊天列的独立覆盖层（会同屏出现两个左栏），改为占用主布局槽位——设置导航列替换左侧栏槽位（含搜索与「返回工作区」），聊天列替换为设置内容区，顶栏不动；48px 拖拽条、扩展 `settings.tab.*` 槽位、分区深链与 Esc 关闭语义全部保持。
  - EN: settings now reuses the main window skeleton: instead of an overlay covering the chat column (which produced two side-by-side nav columns), the settings nav occupies the left sidebar slot (with search and "back to workspace") and the settings content takes the chat column, top bar untouched; the 48px drag strip, extension `settings.tab.*` slots, section deep links, and Esc-to-close all preserved.
- **消息流轮次分隔头不再显示「第 N 轮」编号**：该编号是相对当前已加载窗口的下标，与右侧轮次导航条（daemon 全量索引）在长会话/翻页/分支场景必然不一致；编号移除后导航条是唯一权威轮次计数，分隔头保留模型、时间、状态与耗时。
  - EN: turn separator headers in the transcript no longer show the "Turn N" number: it was an index relative to the currently loaded window, which necessarily disagreed with the round rail's daemon-wide count on long sessions, paging, or branch switches; the rail is now the sole authoritative turn counter while the header keeps model, time, status, and duration.

### Fixed

- **语音模型下载多源镜像回退**：STT/TTS 模型下载此前硬编码 `huggingface.co`（sherpa 文件直取、transformers.js/Kokoro 均无任何镜像配置），大陆网络下模型永远下不来、语音输入与朗读首次使用即失败——这是语音功能"残废"的真正主因。新增 `tiny/hub-mirrors`：默认上游 + `hf-mirror.com` 双源，进程级 HEAD 探测选源（探测胜出者优先、其余保序回退，单文件级失败也跨源重试），transformers.js 侧在首个 pipeline 前设置 `env.remoteHost` 指向探测胜出的 HF 兼容源；`MUSEPI_HUB_ORIGINS` 环境变量可整体覆盖源清单（企业内网镜像可用）。
  - EN: speech-model downloads now fall back across hub origins: STT/TTS fetches were hardcoded to `huggingface.co` (raw sherpa files plus the transformers.js/Kokoro default with no mirror anywhere), so under mainland networks the models never arrived and first-use dictation/TTS always failed — the actual root cause of the "broken" voice features. New `tiny/hub-mirrors`: upstream + `hf-mirror.com` by default, a process-level HEAD probe picks the reachable origin (winner first, the rest kept in order as per-file fallbacks), transformers.js gets `env.remoteHost` pointed at the winning HF-compatible origin before the first pipeline, and `MUSEPI_HUB_ORIGINS` overrides the whole origin list (corporate mirrors included).
- **设置壳层与主界面外壳色差**：设置导航槽与设置视图在 `.gui-main` 的玻璃层之上又各自叠刷了一遍 scrim（深色模式 88% 叠 88%），设置界面外壳明显深于主界面；两个表面改为透明，与侧栏同例共享 `.gui-main` 的单层玻璃，分隔仍由圆角卡片承担。
  - EN: settings chrome vs main UI color mismatch: the settings nav slot and settings view each repainted the glass scrim on top of `.gui-main`'s identical layer (88% over 88% in dark mode), so the settings chrome read visibly darker; both surfaces are now transparent and share `.gui-main`'s single glass layer like the sidebar, with separation still carried by the rounded card.
- **通知派发异常不再中断会话刷新**：`dispatchNotification` 的能力探测混用 `"Notification" in window` 与裸全局构造器，在两者不一致的环境（如 DOM 垫片）抛 TypeError 并中断 session store 的帧合并 flush（批次半应用、快照不更新）；改为一致地从 `window` 读取构造器，通知层异常不再波及会话状态。
  - EN: notification dispatch no longer stalls session refreshes: `dispatchNotification` probed `"Notification" in window` but dereferenced the bare global — where the two disagree (e.g. a DOM shim) it threw a TypeError that killed the session store's frame-coalescing flush mid-batch; the constructor is now read consistently from `window`, so notification-layer quirks can't corrupt session state.
- **会话撤回/分支切换全面修复**：撤回、编辑、重试、分支切换此前在 GUI 上不同程度失效——daemon `session.branchAt` 成功后不产生任何事件推送，GUI 会话视图只能停在旧状态；首条用户消息的撤回在客户端就被 null parentId 拦截（误报「分支切换失败」）；纯线性会话撤回后旧尾巴因过滤前提（须已分叉）不满足而原样显示。现在：branchAt 成功即经 journal 广播 `session_leaf_moved` 事件（真 seq，断线可 catchup 重放），客户端收到后重取快照并对齐水位；撤回首条消息合法化（leaf 落到根，文本回填输入框，TUI resetLeaf 对齐）；消息流过滤改为以 leaf pin 为准，撤回后旧尾巴立即消失。
  - EN: session revert/branch switching fully fixed: revert, edit, retry, and branch switch all failed in various ways in the GUI — a successful daemon `session.branchAt` produced no event push, leaving the GUI session view stuck on the old state; rewinding the first user message was rejected client-side on its null parentId (a bogus "branch switch failed" toast); and in a purely linear session the dropped tail kept rendering because the transcript filter required an existing fork. Now: a successful branchAt broadcasts a journaled `session_leaf_moved` event (real seq, replayable via catchup after a disconnect), clients refetch the snapshot and re-align their watermark; rewinding the first message is legitimate (leaf lands on the root, text backfills the composer, TUI resetLeaf parity); transcript filtering keys on the leaf pin, so the dropped tail disappears immediately after a revert.
- **客户端断档消息静默丢失/乱序修复**：会话事件流统一为 journal 单一权威 seq（重启续 seq、torn 尾部容忍、compact 阈值改自上次 compact 追加数，消灭计数/journal 偏移），新增 `session.catchup {afterSeq}` RPC 按序补推（水位于 checkpoint 之前或尾部之后返回 `resyncRequired`）；桌面 `GuiSessionStore` 加水位闸——重叠不重放、断档显式重订阅、10s 无进展或需 resync 时整快照兜底，断线/丢帧期间不再静默丢消息或乱序。
  - EN: client gap message silent loss/ordering fix: the session event stream is now a single journal-authoritative seq (restart resumes seq, torn tail tolerated, compact threshold switched to appends-since-last-compact, killing the count/journal offset), with a new `session.catchup {afterSeq}` RPC pushing in order (returns `resyncRequired` when the watermark falls before checkpoint or past the tail); desktop `GuiSessionStore` adds a watermark gate — no replay on overlap, explicit resubscribe on gap, full snapshot fallback after 10s stall or when resync is required — so messages no longer silently drop or reorder during disconnects/frame loss.

## [0.4.38] - 2026-09-23

### Fixed

- **设置面板壳层修复（三迭代收口）**：0.4.37 后设置界面的布局问题一次性收口——设置视图改为覆盖「聊天列」（含 header）的覆盖层，应用侧栏同屏完整可见；SettingsView 自带的 48px 拖拽条与圆角卡片完整保留，与会话主界面同一壳层语言（zcode RootShell `isSettingsTabActive` 同构），不再退化成会话容器内的裸页，半透明玻璃导航也不再压住侧栏会话列表；顺带修复设置面板控件列 46px 对齐错位。
  - EN: settings shell fixed (three iterations closed): the settings view became an overlay covering the chat column (header included) with the app sidebar fully visible beside it — SettingsView's own 48px drag bar and rounded card restored, the same shell language as the chat surface (zcode RootShell `isSettingsTabActive` isomorph), no longer a bare page inside the session container, and the translucent glass nav no longer paints over the sidebar's session list; also fixed the 46px control-column misalignment in the settings panel.
- **浮动滚动条层级跟随所在容器**：滚动条栈之前固定挂在最高层，直接盖住更新弹窗（z 900）等所有上层弹层；现在 stackZ 跟随其所属容器的内容层级，容器在上层、滚动条就在上层。
  - EN: floating scrollbar stacking now follows its container: the rail used to sit at a global top layer, painting over the update dialog (z 900) and every upper layer; the stackZ now derives from the owning container's content level.
- **桌宠气泡窗口鸡生蛋死锁**：pet 主窗与 bubbles 气泡窗启动时互相等待对方的创建结果，任一窗口未就绪都会把另一条链挂在 await 上，导致气泡可能永远不出现；创建次序改为单向依赖，启动即稳定出泡。
  - EN: a chicken-and-egg deadlock between the pet main window and the bubbles window: each awaited the other's creation at startup, so a single not-ready window parked the other chain forever and bubbles might never show; creation order is now a one-way dependency and bubbles appear reliably at launch.

## [0.4.37] - 2026-09-23

### Added

- **M1 轮渲染语义落地（ZCode 语义吸收）+ guest-client 归位 client-core**：会话消息渲染从消息级平铺改为轮级投影——与折叠/导航/轨迹同一 `isTurnStart` 口径，轮卡片承载用户请求、assistant 回复、工具调用与思考过程；guest-client（远程网页/安卓）渲染组件归位到共享的 `client-core`，与 desktop-app 同一套轮语义实现，不再各写一份。
  - EN: M1 turn-rendering semantics landed (ZCode semantics absorbed): session rendering moved from a flat message list to a turn-level projection — same `isTurnStart` caliber as folding/navigation/trajectory, with turn cards carrying the user request, assistant reply, tool calls and thinking; the guest-client (remote web / Android) rendering moved into the shared `client-core`, one turn-semantic implementation for both desktop-app and guest-client instead of two.
- **轮级会话地图（TurnMapCanvas）**：9985 条消息级事件投影为轮级画布——164 轮 = 164 张玻璃节点卡，主线是垂直时间轴（金色流动渐变连线），重答/分叉轮横向开列（金→紫渐变支线），顾问轮薄荷青徽章；单击展开轮内事件泳道，双击跳回对话定位该轮；左侧迷你导航条 = 全轮次剪影（视口映射 + 点击跳转）。消息级画布（SessionTreeCanvas）降级为调试入口。附带修复全量历史补全后地图/轨迹只见尾窗的问题（entries 身份切换时重新适配视野）。
  - EN: turn-level session map (TurnMapCanvas): 9985 message-level events projected onto a turn canvas — 164 turns = 164 glass node cards, main line a vertical timeline (flowing gold gradient), retry/branch turns open new columns (gold→purple gradient side lanes), advisor turns in mint; single-click expands the turn's event lanes, double-click jumps back to the transcript at that turn; the left minimap strip shows the full-turn silhouette (viewport mapping + click-to-jump). The message-level canvas (SessionTreeCanvas) is demoted to a debug entry. Also fixed the map/trajectory showing only the trailing window after full-history backfill (viewport re-fits when the entries identity switches).

### Changed

- **输入框 kimicode 版式吸收 + 可编辑快捷键注册表面板**：输入框「+」弹层吸收 Kimi Code 设计（截屏 / 各功能说明 / 快捷键 chip 展示）；新增快捷键注册表面板（设置 → 快捷键，可视化编辑绑定）；修复画板形状弹窗两个实测问题（按钮只开不收、图案点击选不中）；长会话 keeper 常驻与轨迹口径收口。
  - EN: the composer "+" popover absorbs the Kimi Code layout (screenshot / per-feature descriptions / shortcut chips); a new shortcut registry panel (Settings → Shortcuts) edits bindings visually; two live issues in the shape popover fixed (button only opened, never closed; patterns couldn't be selected by click); long-session keeper stays resident and the trajectory caliber is tightened.
- **轨迹视图长会话优化**：事件数 > 400 的会话进入轨迹面板时全部轮次默认折叠（render 期播种一次，手动展开状态不被重置），mode 行新增「折叠全部 / 展开全部」；分支树渐进挂载（首帧 300 行，IntersectionObserver 哨兵每次追加 500）；时间线 ↔ 分支树切换走 useTransition + 不确定进度条；区间跳转 rAF + setTimeout 双保险（窗口被遮挡 rAF 停摆也能落定）；14 处裸值 border-radius 按 gui-design.md 阶梯收口为 token 变量。
  - EN: trajectory-panel long-session optimization: sessions with >400 events enter with all turns folded (render-time one-shot seeding, manual expand state preserved) plus "fold all / expand all" in the mode row; the branch tree mounts progressively (300 rows first, IntersectionObserver sentinel appends 500 at a time); timeline↔tree switching uses useTransition with an indeterminate progress bar; range jumps get an rAF + setTimeout double safeguard (still lands when the window is occluded and rAF stalls); 14 raw border-radius values closed into the gui-design.md阶梯 token variables.
- **轮级地图视口裁剪 + memo 节点卡**：只渲染可见世界矩形（外扩 260px）内的节点卡，展开轮高度参与命中计算；实机 164 轮会话聚焦态 DOM 卡从 164 降到 13，导航条 164 band 剪影不受影响；TmNodeCard memo 化 + 轮内统计 useMemo，挡住 hover 浮卡/搜索高亮的整树重渲染。
  - EN: turn-map viewport culling + memoized node cards: only node cards inside the visible world rect (260px pad) render, with expanded-turn height in the hit test; on a live 164-turn session the focused-state DOM cards drop from 164 to 13 while the 164-band minimap silhouette is unaffected; TmNodeCard is memoized with per-turn stats useMemo, blocking whole-tree rerenders from hover cards / search highlights.

### Fixed

- **桌宠消息气泡拆分为独立窗口**：2026-09-16 的单窗口合并让气泡在 pet 窗内绝对定位——栈体从窗口底边向上生长、无视工作区边界，屏幕右缘/顶缘直接被裁切，窗口还会压住主客户端。现在气泡回到自己的窗口（`bubbles.html`）：渲染端上报内容尺寸/占用状态/卡片命中区，主进程把窗口精确设为内容大小、底部钉在精灵头顶上方 20px、水平对角色角色中心，并做工作区钳制（多显示器按角色所在屏）；拖拽/停靠/吸附/显示变更全部跟随（`setPetBounds` 单一咽喉点）；空栈隐藏窗口，透明外边距环与卡片间隙保持点击穿透（与桌宠同一套 120ms 轮询）。
  - EN: the pet message bubbles got their own window again (the 2026-09-16 single-window merge absolutely positioned them inside the pet window — the stack grew upward from the window's bottom edge with no work-area awareness, clipping at screen edges and overlapping the main client). The renderer reports its content size, occupancy and card hitbox; the main process sizes the window to exactly the content, pins its bottom edge 20px above the sprite's top centred on the character with work-area clamping (per-display), follows every drag/settle/dock/display change via the `setPetBounds` choke point, hides the window on an empty stack, and keeps the transparent padding ring + card gaps click-through (same 120ms poll as the pet).
- **会话注册引导的并发竞态（daemon 静默死亡的元凶之一）**：`registry.ensure` 与 `sessions.create` 可同时触发首次引导，两条链各自 `createAgentSession` 并互相 `dispose`（后写者把先写者的会话从注册表挤掉并销毁其全局生命周期），在引导风暴下 daemon 会静默退出。现在引导全程持有互斥锁（in-flight promise 去重），引导会话改用子级任务前缀（`registry-bootstrap-N`），不再抢占全局 "Main" 任务及其生命周期。
  - EN: a concurrency race in session-registry bootstrap (one of the causes of the daemon's silent death): `registry.ensure` and `sessions.create` could both trigger the first bootstrap, each calling `createAgentSession` and `dispose`-ing the other — the later writer evicted the earlier session from the registry and destroyed its global lifecycle, and under a bootstrap storm the daemon silently exited. The bootstrap now runs under a mutex (in-flight promise dedup) and uses a child task prefix (`registry-bootstrap-N`) instead of claiming the global "Main" task and its lifecycle.
- **协同弹窗（CollabDialog）关闭态渲染循环风暴**：DialogFrame 关闭时子组件 hooks 照跑，未记忆化的 refresh 回调让每个渲染帧都重触发 boot effect，实测 13 秒内发出 689 次 `channels.list`/`plugins.list` RPC。refresh/refreshChannels/refreshPlugins 已 `useCallback` 化，轮询与 boot effect 均以 `open` 门控。
  - EN: a render-loop storm in the CollabDialog while closed: DialogFrame keeps running child hooks when closed, and unmemoized refresh callbacks re-triggered the boot effect every render frame — 689 `channels.list`/`plugins.list` RPCs in 13s observed live. The refresh callbacks are now `useCallback`-memoized and both the polling and the boot effect are gated on `open`.
- **渠道插件扫描无缓存**：`loadChannelPlugins` 每次调用都重新扫描目录，风暴期间被数百次重复调用。现在带 2s TTL 缓存（`force` 可绕过，登录等路径仍拿新鲜结果）。
  - EN: channel-plugin scanning had no cache — the loader re-scanned the directory on every call and was repeated hundreds of times during the storm. A 2s TTL cache now backs it (`force` bypasses it for login-path freshness).

### Changed

- **daemon RPC 追踪开关**：设置 `MUSEPI_RPC_TRACE=1` 环境变量后，daemon 对每条 RPC 请求记录 `rpc.request` 日志（方法名），用于排查 RPC 风暴与静默死亡；默认关闭，零开销。
  - EN: a daemon RPC trace switch — with `MUSEPI_RPC_TRACE=1` the daemon logs an `rpc.request` entry (method name) per request, for storm/silent-death diagnosis; off by default, zero cost.

- **桌宠主题调色板从未真正生效（变量名双前缀），品牌金观感偏差**：`applyPetPalette` 写入的是 `--gui-pet-gui-pet-shell-a`（前缀 `--gui-pet-` 叠加键名自带的 `gui-pet-`），而 SVG 读取的是 `var(--gui-pet-shell-a, …)`——派生调色板自 2026-09-20 上线起整体静默落空，桌宠永远停在 SVG 硬编码回退色上。前缀修正为 `--` 后主题色（品牌金/海洋/翡翠/单色）实时跟随。同时把静默 catch 改为 `console.warn`，以后调色板再失败控制台可见。已用实机验证脚本（`verify-desktop-pet.mjs`，15 项断言）锁定写入契约。
  - EN: the pet theme palette never actually applied — `applyPetPalette` wrote `--gui-pet-gui-pet-shell-a` (the `--gui-pet-` prefix stacked on keys that already carry `gui-pet-`) while the SVG reads `var(--gui-pet-shell-a, …)`, so the derived palette silently fell through to the hardcoded SVG fallbacks since it shipped on 2026-09-20. The prefix is corrected to `--`; the silent catch now logs a `console.warn`. Locked by a live verification script (`verify-desktop-pet.mjs`, 15 assertions).
- **petdex 主题包在桌宠窗口不生效**：petdex 包存在主窗口 localStorage，桌宠窗在 `file://` 下读不到，切了主题包桌宠却不变。现在主窗口推送 `pet-activity` 时内联活动宠物描述符（内建 id 或 petdex 包，含 spritesheet），桌宠窗按推送渲染。
  - EN: petdex pet packages live in the main window's localStorage, unreachable from the pet window under `file://` — switching the package left the desktop pet unchanged. The main window now inlines the active-pet descriptor (builtin id or petdex package with spritesheet) into its `pet-activity` pushes, and the pet window renders from the push.

### Changed

- **桌宠单击改为前台显示客户端窗口（单击弹窗删除）**：单击的延迟动作从「弹出气泡面板」改为「greeting 反应 + 聚焦主窗」，双击仍是随机交互；右键菜单的「显示/隐藏面板」项一并移除，旧 `pet-toggle-panel` IPC 全链路删除。
  - EN: the desktop pet's single-click action now greets and raises the client window instead of popping the bubble panel (panel removed, `pet-toggle-panel` IPC deleted end-to-end); double-click still plays a random interaction.
- **气泡悬停快捷操作 + 内联回复**：气泡悬停浮出操作条——✓ 确认（审批通过 / 完成已读 / 关闭）、✗ 拒绝（仅审批）、💬 回复（带会话的气泡，点击展开内联输入框，Enter 发送走 `pet-reply`）；展开态头部新增「清除全部」，底部 ∨ 圆形按钮收起。旧气泡面板 DOM/CSS 全量移除。
  - EN: hovering a pet bubble reveals an action bar — confirm (approve / mark-read / close), deny (approvals only) and reply (session bubbles only; expands an inline input, Enter sends via `pet-reply`). The expanded list gains a "clear all" header action and a ∨ collapse fab. The old bubble panel DOM/CSS is fully gone.
- **品牌金重校为香槟金 + 点击挤压动效**：暗色/浅色两套金色公式全面降饱和（如暗色 gold-b 从 `oklch(75.07% 0.1450 80)` 调至 `oklch(81.07% 0.1101 79.84)`），金属坡 ramp 保持高光洗白/本体峰值/暗部留色的非单调色度结构；窗口级 squash 挤压动效（blobstudio 式）在每次交互反应时重放，petdex 精灵表没有反应帧行，挤压即可见反馈。
  - EN: the brand-gold ramp is recalibrated champagne — both schemes desaturated (e.g. dark gold-b from `oklch(75.07% 0.1450 80)` to `oklch(81.07% 0.1101 79.84)`) while keeping the polished-metal non-monotonic chroma structure; a blobstudio-style window squash plays on every interaction reaction (petdex sheets carry no reaction rows, so the squash is the visible feedback).
- **GUI 供应商登录：授权完成后报「request timeout: providers.login」，登录面板标题显示内部 id**：`providers.login` 走的是 WebSocket RPC 默认 15 秒超时，而 OAuth 需要用户在浏览器里完成授权，必然超时——daemon 侧的登录流程其实还在正常跑，GUI 却把整个登录态覆盖成一条原始报错，授权链接和取消按钮都没了。现在登录请求给到 5 分钟超时（仍可取消），出错时保留授权链接/说明，面板标题从内部 id（如 `kimi-code`）改为供应商显示名；等待授权的转圈此前被全局 busy 标志反向 gate 住（登录进行中反而不显示），现改为按该供应商的 pending 状态显示。引导页（Onboarding）登录同样受益。
  - EN: GUI provider login showed "request timeout: providers.login" after the user authorized, and the panel title displayed the internal id. `providers.login` used the WebSocket RPC's default 15s timeout, but OAuth needs the user to finish authorizing in a browser — it always timed out while the daemon flow kept running fine; the GUI then replaced the whole login state with a raw error, losing the auth link and cancel button. The login request now gets a 5-minute timeout (still cancellable), errors keep the auth link/instructions, and the panel title shows the provider display name instead of the internal id (e.g. `kimi-code`). The "waiting for authorization" spinner was also gated backwards by the global busy flag (hidden while logging in) — it now follows that provider's pending state. Onboarding logins benefit from the same fixes.

## [0.4.36] - 2026-09-20

### Added

- **IM 渠道打通回复链路：微信里发一句话，agent 的回答终于会回来**：此前渠道侧只有命令回显（/help 等），纯文本被送进会话后 agent 跑完就没有任何推送路径——用户看到的是"发出去石沉大海"，体感就是 bot 很慢/死了。现在会话结束时把最后一条 assistant 回复推回每个绑定的 IM 端；绑定按「渠道+发送者」记录并存盘，重启后照样路由。未绑定的第一条消息不再报错教育，而是自动新建会话并直接开始处理（对齐 Telegram DM：第一句话就有回应）。
  - EN: IM channels now actually answer back. Previously only command echoes existed — a plain text message reached the session and the agent's reply had no push path at all, so the bot looked dead/slow. The last assistant reply is now pushed to every bound IM peer when a turn ends, and bindings are keyed per channel+sender and persisted, so routing survives a restart. An unbound first message no longer returns an error lecture: it auto-creates a session and starts working (Telegram-DM parity — the first message already gets an answer).
- **各渠道按自身原生能力适配**：微信接入 iLink 原生「对方正在输入」（getconfig 取 typing_ticket，5 秒心跳，回复时收尾）；Telegram 用 sendChatAction 输入状态 + HTML 富文本渲染（代码块/粗体/链接，解析失败自动回落纯文本）并接收文档与语音占位；Discord 用 REST typing 端点并把非图片附件标注出来；飞书首个分片以「引用回复」落到原消息下（群里看得出在答谁，飞书没有 bot 输入态接口，故不伪装）。所有渠道的长回复改为分片发送，不再静默截断丢尾；命令回复语言按渠道走（微信/飞书/lark/华为中文，Telegram/Discord 英文），/list 显示序号+短 id，手机上 `/switch 2` 就能绑定。
  - EN: Each channel is adapted to what it natively supports: WeChat gets iLink's native "typing…" (typing_ticket via getconfig, 5s heartbeat, cleared on reply); Telegram gets sendChatAction typing plus HTML rendering (code blocks, bold, links — plain-text fallback if the parser rejects it) and now surfaces documents/voice placeholders; Discord gets the REST typing endpoint and annotates non-image attachments; Feishu quotes the source message for the first chunk (so a group can tell what is being answered) and does NOT fake typing, since bots have no input-state API there. Long replies are chunked everywhere instead of being silently truncated, command replies follow the channel's language (Chinese for WeChat/Feishu/Lark/Huawei, English for Telegram/Discord), and /list prints ordinals + short ids so `/switch 2` is typeable on a phone.

### Changed

- **飞书/Lark 的回复从「首片引用 + 后续平发」改成整段线程**：此前只有第一个分片挂在被回复的那条消息下，其余分片平发，长回复在群里散成一堆互不相关的气泡；而且引用的是「最近一条入站消息」，agent 跑 30 秒期间有人插话，回复就挂到那句无关的话下面了。现在消息 id 随入站一路带到回复侧（各渠道统一走 `meta.messageId`），首片引用触发它的原消息，后续分片挂到 bot 自己发的首条上——整段回复是一条可折叠的线程；发送改为串行并按 220ms 节流对齐群机器人 5 QPS。
  - EN: Feishu/Lark replies are now one thread instead of "first chunk quoted, rest flat". Only the first chunk used to hang under the answered message, so a long reply scattered into unrelated bubbles — and it quoted "the newest inbound message", meaning a message sent while the agent was running got the whole answer attached to it. The message id now travels from the inbound path all the way to the reply (a single `meta.messageId` across channels): the first chunk quotes the message that triggered the turn, later chunks hang off the bot's own first message, so the whole reply is one collapsible thread. Sends are serial and throttled to 220ms to stay under the group bot's ~5 QPS.

- **GUI 渠道行：点「启动」直接给二维码**：wechat 的 token 是可选的（空值走扫码登录），此前点启动先展开一个表单、必须再点「保存并启动」才出二维码，属于多一层无意义的操作。现在字段全可选的渠道点启动直接连接，二维码立刻出现；想预置 token 走齿轮入口。
  - EN: The GUI channel row now gives the QR straight away. WeChat's token is optional (empty means QR login), but starting used to expand a form first and demanded a second "save and start" click before any QR appeared — one pointless extra step. Channels whose fields are all optional now connect immediately, and the token form lives behind a gear button.

### Fixed

- **Discord：群聊里消息根本收不到，图片附件还被静默丢弃**：两个各自致命的问题。① IDENTIFY 只声明了 GUILD_MESSAGES | DIRECT_MESSAGES，缺 MESSAGE_CONTENT（1<<15）——这是 privileged intent，缺它时群里**没有 @bot** 的消息 content 恒为空串，进到命令路由就被当空消息丢掉，表现是"群里说话 bot 不理、私聊正常"。② 入站回调签名只有三个参数，`#handleMessage` 下载并编码好的图片在 attach 时被整个吞掉——用户发图，会话只收到文字。现在补上 intent（需在 Developer Portal 一并打开开关，否则 4014）并让回调透传附件；两个都补了回归测试。顺带修掉带图/带文件时正文被 `slice(0, 2000)` 静默截断（分片白做了）。
  - EN: Discord could not hear group chats at all, and silently dropped image attachments — two separate fatal bugs. (1) IDENTIFY declared only GUILD_MESSAGES | DIRECT_MESSAGES, missing MESSAGE_CONTENT (1<<15), a privileged intent: without it, any guild message that does NOT @mention the bot arrives with an EMPTY `content` and is discarded as a blank message, so the bot answered in DMs and looked dead in channels. (2) The inbound callback took three arguments, so the images `#handleMessage` had already downloaded and base64-encoded were swallowed at attach() — a picture arrived as text only. The intent is declared now (it must also be toggled in the Developer Portal or IDENTIFY is rejected with 4014) and the callback forwards attachments; both have regression tests. Text sent with attachments is no longer silently sliced at 2000 either — chunking was being undone at that branch.
- **Telegram：限流被当成渲染失败处理，一条回复会发两遍**：发送失败原先一律回落到「去掉 HTML 再发一次」。HTML 解析失败这样处理是对的，但 429 限流走同一条路就变成重发——用户收到两条一模一样的消息，而且把限流压得更狠；getUpdates 侧同样没有退避，409（另一个实例在轮询这个 token）时按固定 1.2s 死循环空转。现在按 Telegram 自己的语义分流：429 读 `retry_after` 等待后重发，只有 400 且明确抱怨实体解析时才回落到纯文本，轮询错误按指数退避（上限 60s）。
  - EN: Telegram treated rate limiting as a rendering failure, so one reply went out twice. Every send failure used to fall back to "resend without HTML" — correct for a rejected entity, wrong for a 429: the message was resent as a duplicate and the limit deepened. getUpdates had no backoff either, spinning at a fixed 1.2s on a 409 (another instance polling the same token). It now follows Telegram's own semantics: a 429 waits out `retry_after` and resends, only a 400 explicitly complaining about entity parsing drops the markup, and polling errors back off exponentially (capped at 60s).
- **飞书：长回复的分片会撞限流，一片失败整段回复就没了**：4 个分片瞬间连发，群机器人 5 QPS 一撞就返回 230098，而循环里没有任何容错——第一个分片失败就抛错，后面几个全丢，用户只看到半截答案。现在每个分片独立重试并回落平发，失败只记日志、其余照发，只有全部失败才报错；分片之间按 220ms 节流。
  - EN: Feishu dropped whole replies when chunks hit the rate limit. Four chunks went out back to back, tripping the group bot's ~5 QPS (code 230098), and the loop had no fault tolerance at all — one rejected chunk threw and the rest were never sent, leaving the user with half an answer. Each chunk now retries on its own and falls back to a plain send; failures are logged and the remaining chunks still go out, and an error is raised only if every chunk is rejected. Chunks are throttled 220ms apart.
- **长回复会在代码块中间被切开，以及 Discord 断线后不再重连**：① 分片只看换行/空格，切在 ``` 围栏中间时两半各带一个不成对的围栏标记，渲染出来是裸的 ``` 和一堆没格式的代码（agent 回复几乎必带代码块，长回复必现）。现在切点会避开围栏：跨片的代码块在本片闭合、下一片原样重开。② Discord gateway 只处理了 Hello/READY，没有 op 7 Reconnect、op 9 Invalid Session、resume（op 6），也没做 heartbeat ack 检测——网络抖一下就是永久下线，只能手动重启。现在断线按指数退避自动重连（有 session 就 resume，没有才重新 IDENTIFY），连续两个心跳没收到 ack 即判定僵尸连接并重建。
  - EN: Long replies were cut in half inside code blocks, and Discord never reconnected. (1) Chunking only looked for newlines/spaces, so a cut inside a ``` fence left both halves with an unpaired marker — rendered as a bare ``` followed by unformatted code (agent answers almost always contain code, so any long reply showed it). Cut points now avoid fences: a block spanning chunks is closed at the end of one and reopened at the start of the next. (2) The Discord gateway handled only Hello/READY — no op 7 Reconnect, no op 9 Invalid Session, no resume (op 6), and no heartbeat-ack watchdog, so a single network blip meant permanent offline until a manual restart. Drops now reconnect with exponential backoff (resuming when a session survives, identifying only when it does not), and two unacked heartbeats in a row are treated as a zombie connection and rebuilt.

- **「停止」不再等于解绑：扫码拿到的凭证会落盘，停止/解绑两个动作分开**：QR 登录拿到的 bot_token 此前从不写回 channels.json，于是停止后再启动必须重新扫码——用户以为点的是「停止」，实际是解绑。现在 token 落盘，停止只断线；真正的解绑是独立按钮，且先弹二次确认（Enter 确认 / Esc 取消）。
  - EN: "Stop" no longer means unbind. The bot_token from a QR login was never written back to channels.json, so stopping and starting again demanded a fresh scan — clicking what read as "stop" was effectively unbinding. The credential is persisted now, stop only disconnects, and unbinding is a separate button behind a confirm dialog (Enter confirms, Escape cancels).
- **长回复不再被静默截断**：微信 2000、Telegram 4096、Discord 2000、飞书 4000 处的 slice() 把超长回复的尾巴直接丢掉，agent 的回答会在半句话处突然结束；现全部改为分片发送。
  - EN: Long replies are no longer silently truncated: a slice() at 2000 (WeChat), 4096 (Telegram), 2000 (Discord) and 4000 (Feishu) dropped the tail of long answers, ending them mid-sentence. All channels now send chunks instead.

## [0.4.35] - 2026-09-20

### Added

- **桌宠状态轴原文级吸收 blobstudio：31 态运动 + 七类特效，导入 SVG 伙伴获得同一套系统**：放弃"语义相似"的自写渲染，从站点 bundle 反解出的引擎源码逐字吸收——运动表六处值（dash/bob/sway/pulse 的半径、周期、缩放）与源码字面量一致，dash 飞行是 Lissajous 8 字（cos r / sin 2r·0.44），身体飞行与轨迹尾巴焊在同一个逐入场时钟上（换持续相位钟头尾就会脱节）；七类粒子特效（轨迹双层遮挡、dots 三次脉冲、glyph 真 path、徽标等）为渲染函数逐帧移植，特效层是运动 rig 的**兄弟层**——继承身体 transform 会把 dash 头甩到两倍偏移。dots/glyph 特效直接驱动共享身体组做"让位消解"（SVG 与 HTML 双通道同一公式）。配套的状态判定（会话信号 → 31 态）、表情池与节奏表同步落地，`pet-state.test` 钉死全部字面量。
  - EN: The pet's state axis is a verbatim absorption of blobstudio: 31-state motion plus seven effect families, with imported SVG companions getting the same system. Semantic re-creation is out — the engine source recovered from the site bundle is absorbed literally: the six motion-table values (dash/bob/sway/pulse radius, period, scale) match the source word for word, the dash flight is the Lissajous figure-eight (cos r / sin 2r·0.44), and the body's flight and its trail share ONE per-entry clock (a persistent phase clock would leave head and tail at different loop points). The seven particle effects are per-frame ports of the render functions, and the effect layers are SIBLINGS of the motion rig — inheriting the body's transform flings the dash head to twice the flight offset. The dots/glyph effects drive the shared body group directly for the yield-dissolve (SVG and HTML channels, same formula). Session-signal state resolution, expression pools and cadence tables landed with it, and `pet-state.test` pins every literal.
- **导入的 SVG 伙伴升级为完整伙伴**：`format:"svg"` 的 petdex 包此前退化到单帧 transform 行情；现在经 PetdexStateStage 渲染，吃满 31 态运动、七类特效与身体消解，全部按 face-unit（228.541 盒）归一——任意宿主尺寸下运动振幅与特效坐标保持源码比例。这正是源站自己的契约："上传的 logo 与内置圆球行为完全一致"。纯导入页的特效填充渐变缺 defs，overlay 内逐字补齐同名 fallback。
  - EN: Imported SVG companions become full companions: a `format:"svg"` petdex package used to degrade to the single-frame transform row; it now renders through PetdexStateStage and receives the 31-state motion, the seven effect families and the body dissolve, all normalized in face units (the 228.541 box) so proportion survives any host size. This is the source site's own contract — "an uploaded logo behaves exactly like the built-in circle". The effects' fill gradient had no defs on pure-import pages; the overlay re-declares an identical fallback verbatim.

### Changed

- **耳机重制为 AirPods Max（白 + 玫瑰金），并整体加大**：反解确认源站没有配饰系统——一切附属物都活在身体组里，耳机随之整体住进 bodyRef：dash 飞行带着耳机走、dots 消解连球带耳机一起化掉，不再是一堆硬件悬在彗星头上。配色钉死为白色阳极氧化板 + 玫瑰金轨/滑杆/Digital Crown（accent 派生会溶进金壳；炭灰版被读作秃头；AirPods Max 没有麦克风臂，Crown 才是签名）。整套耳机绕球心放大 1.08（并入引擎每帧配件 transform，1.10 的 canopy 圆帽会出 rig 界）；挂靠输入框的小球上限 46→52、下限 34→38（rig padding 卸载后同一上限读起来偏小）。
  - EN: The headphones are rebuilt as AirPods Max (white + rose gold), and enlarged as one object. Reverse-engineering confirmed the source site has NO accessory system — everything attached to the body lives in the body group, so the headset now rides bodyRef wholesale: dash flights carry it, the dots dissolve take it along with the ball, instead of hardware hovering over a comet. The palette is pinned to anodized-white plates + rose-gold rails/arms/Digital Crown (accent-derived colour dissolved into the gold shell; the charcoal interim read as bald; AirPods Max has no mic arm — the Crown is the signature). The whole headset scales 1.08 about the sphere centre (inside the engine's per-frame accessory transform; 1.10 pushes the canopy caps past the rig). The composer-docked orb's clamp moves 46→52 (floor 34→38) — with the rig padding shed, the same cap read undersized.
- **小球定制搬到常规页 Agent 头像区**：球面光泽开关与配饰选择器从伴侣页尾部迁到常规页头像网格正下方——宠物头像卡就是活的 BuiltinPetSprite，改动在预览旁即时可见。组件自持状态并订阅 omp-pet-changed 重解析，挂哪都成立；伴侣页保留形象/尺寸/导入。
  - EN: The ball's customization moves to the GENERAL page's agent-avatar area: the gloss toggle and accessory picker leave the companion page's tail for a slot right under the avatar grid — the pet avatar card is a live BuiltinPetSprite, so changes read back beside the preview. The section is self-contained and re-resolves on omp-pet-changed, so it works wherever it mounts; the companion page keeps pick/size/import.

### Fixed

- **画板文字标签：六个字只显示三个、以及拖不动角手柄**：两个独立的真 bug。① 盒宽按「字符数 × 字号 × 0.62」估算，那是拉丁文的平均字宽——汉字步进约 1.0em，要宽约 1.6 倍，于是六个 24px 汉字被「估算」成 89px 而实际占 ~144px，盒子被夹到最小宽度、中文标签只露出前三个字。现在宽度取自**真实画布度量**（`measureTextWidth`，与 Konva 自己量文字用的是同一种操作），中文、英文、混排一律按实际绘制宽度算，一行放不下就换行、盒子向下增高。② 上一轮从手柄的**渲染**条件里删掉了「排除文字」，却漏了 `onPointerDown` 里同一个闸门——手柄画出来了，但按下时命中没有登记缩放状态，拖动成为空操作。两处现已同时移除，文字与其他图形走完全相同的缩放/拖动/擦除路径。另外：用户亲手拖出的宽度会被**保留**（文字在里面换行，不再弹回去贴合内容）、文字按字断行（中文没有空格，只按词切分会让整句停在一行）、编辑浮层的字体与画布统一（此前提交瞬间文字会重排），并且在无画布环境下度量会降级而不是抛错。
  - EN: Sketch board text labels: six characters showing only three, and corner handles that would not drag — two independent real bugs. (1) Box width was estimated as `chars × fontSize × 0.62`, a Latin average; a CJK glyph advances ~1.0em, about 1.6× wider, so six 24px 汉字 "measured" 89px while occupying ~144px — the box clamped to its minimum and the label showed only its first three characters. Width now comes from a **real canvas metric** (`measureTextWidth`, the same operation Konva itself measures text with), so CJK, Latin and mixed strings are sized by what actually gets painted: anything too long wraps and the box grows downward. (2) The previous pass removed the text exclusion from the handles' **render** condition but missed the identical gate in `onPointerDown` — the handles drew, but the press never registered scale state, so dragging was a no-op. Both are gone now, and text resizes/drags/erases through exactly the path shapes use. Also: a width the user dragged is **kept** (text wraps inside it instead of the box springing back to fit its content), text wraps per glyph (CJK has no spaces, so a word-only splitter left a whole Chinese sentence on one line), the editing overlay shares the canvas font (committing used to visibly reflow the text), and measurement degrades instead of throwing where no canvas exists.

- **画板文字标签随文本自动换行增高，且与其他图形一样可缩放**：旧实现里文字只存一个锚点，宽度每次渲染都用「字符数 × 字号 × 0.62」重算，于是一整句话永远是单行、直接冲出画板、高度永不增长；同时缩放手柄层显式排除文字，导致它是画板上唯一拖不动也缩放不了的对象。现在文字与其他对象**共用同一套对象模型**：标签持有真实矩形（`[x,y,w,h]`），输入时按词换行、高度跟着行数长，宽度回缩到最宽一行（上限是用户拖出的盒宽）；选中后出现与矩形/图片相同的四角手柄，缩放、拖动、擦除全走同一条代码路径；单击＝选中并拖动，**双击**才进入编辑（过去单击即打开插入符，正是它让文字拖不动）。编辑浮层自身也随输入换行增高。存量画板里只有两个数字的旧标签打开时自动升级成盒子，不会错位或丢失。
  - EN: Sketch board text labels now wrap, grow and resize like every other shape. The old label stored a bare anchor and re-derived its width from `charCount × fontSize × 0.62` on every render, so a whole sentence stayed a single line, ran off the board and never grew taller; the scale-handle layer also excluded text outright, making it the one object on the board you could neither drag nor resize. Text now shares the **same object model** as everything else: a label owns a real rectangle (`[x, y, w, h]`), wraps by word as you type, grows taller with its line count and retracts in width to the widest line (capped at the box you dragged), and once selected it shows the same four corner handles as a rect or an image — resize, drag and erase all run down one code path. A single click selects and drags, and **double-click** enters editing (the old single-click caret was exactly what made labels immovable). The editing overlay itself wraps and grows with the text too. Existing boards whose labels hold only two numbers are upgraded to a box on open, so nothing shifts or goes missing.

- **画板：点击画板图重新打开的是原画（A0），不再是粘贴一张导出图（A1）**：此前完成只导出一张 PNG，点回 chip 时又把那张 PNG 当底图挂进画板——每一笔、每个图形和文字全部融成一张位图，再编辑只能在图上涂，任何一处旧细节都改不回来。现在完成同时交出画板的**笔画列表**（`lib/sketch-scene.ts` 的 scene），composer 把它挂在 chip 上：点击 chip 恢复的是对象本身，每一笔依旧可选、可拖、可缩放、可擦除，改哪一处就改哪一处；再次完成是像素与 scene **一起**替换，来回编辑多少次都不退化。恢复出来的内容是画板的开场状态（不进撤销栈、不算未保存改动），笔画 id 从恢复集之后续号以免命中错对象；换窗口尺寸后重开会等比缩放回去（压力值不动，线宽随同一系数）。草稿里也尽量带上 scene（超容量或含导入图片时只丢 scene、保住附件），缺失时回落成旧的整图打开，不会退化成打不开。
  - EN: Sketch board: clicking a board image reopens the original drawing (A0) instead of pasting the exported copy (A1). Finishing used to emit only a PNG, and clicking the chip mounted that PNG back as the base layer — every pen stroke, shape and label fused into one bitmap, so a re-edit could only paint over the picture and no earlier detail was recoverable. 完成 now also hands over the board's **stroke list** (a `scene`, `lib/sketch-scene.ts`) which the composer stores on the chip: clicking it restores the objects themselves, so each stroke is again selectable, movable, scalable and erasable — you fix exactly the part you meant to. Finishing again swaps pixels **and** scene, so the round trip survives any number of re-edits. Restored content is the board's opening state (not an undo step, not a dirty change), stroke ids continue past the restored set so the eraser and select tool never hit the wrong object, and reopening in a resized window rescales proportionally (pressure untouched, thickness follows the same factor). Drafts carry the scene when it fits (oversized payloads or scenes holding an imported picture drop only the scene and keep the attachment), and a missing scene falls back to the old flat-image reopen rather than refusing to open.

## [0.4.34] - 2026-09-20

### Added

- 桌宠新增**交互反应轴**（戳弄 / 被抓包 / 入睡前）：除会话状态那条轴之外，新增第二条轴专管**用户主动触发的反应**——`startled`（戳一下的惊愕：瞪眼、视线收回正中、刻意不眨眼）、`delighted`（900ms 内补一下的开心：弯眼 + 弹跳）、`curious`（察觉时的偏头，视线明显偏出去）、`dozing`（入睡前的半睁眼，全系统最慢最深的呼吸）、`peek`（"被抓包"的假装没看你）。反应**叠在状态之上**而非常规替换：戳弄绝不写进会话状态、按自己的时钟释放（composer 与桌面桌宠同步），并且期间状态那层的材质（error 的红眼、hover 的增亮辉光）不会被重置——所以球在工作时被戳，仍会一边转圈一边惊一下。身体运动只在戳弄类反应里带过冲入场（全系统唯一允许"闹"的运动），约一秒内自解；同时补齐两个闲时桥段：进入 60s 睡眠闩锁前 40s 开始打瞌睡，以及每 12–26s 一次的偏头/"被抓包"。
  - EN: The pet gains an **interaction reaction axis** (poke / caught-looking / pre-sleep): beyond the session-state axis, a second axis now owns **user-initiated reactions** — `startled` (the gape on a poke: eyes fly wide, gaze snaps to centre, deliberately no blink), `delighted` (a follow-up within 900ms: pleased squint plus a bounce), `curious` (the head-tilt of noticing, gaze parked well off-centre), `dozing` (half-lidded pre-sleep, the slowest and deepest breath in the system), and `peek` (the "caught looking" pretence). Reactions LAYER on top of the mood rather than replacing it: a poke is never written to session state, releases on its own clock (identical on the composer pet and the desktop pet), and the mood's materials (error's red eye, hover's brightened glow) survive underneath — poke the orb while it works and it still startles *and* keeps spinning. The only loud body motion in the system lives on the poke reactions (an overshoot entrance that resolves in about a second); two new idle beats came with it — drowse starting 40s before the 60s sleep latch, and a peek/curious beat every 12–26s.

### Fixed

- **语音输入第二轮：中文出简体、转录全程有反馈、麦克风按钮回归动效**：中文识别输出繁体的根因是内置 whisper-small q8 的中文语料偏台湾侧——两条引擎都没有 decoder prompt 可偏置文字方向，现在解码后统一过 OpenCC 繁→简（新模块 `zh-hans.ts`，懒加载约 1 MB 词典，仅在语言钉住 zh 时生效，加载失败降级原文；TW/HK 用 auto 可保留繁体）。「转录期间毫无反馈、文字凭空出现」的根因是 composer 用两个布尔标志维护听写状态，点停止时双双清零而转录请求才刚开始——整个等待期渲染成空闲；重构为单一相位状态机（`use-dictation.ts`：录音→转写→完成），转写中再点一次是真取消（旧版只清 UI、在途转录结果照样落进输入框），Esc 任意相位取消、卸载自动清理。反馈形态按裁定重做：30px 麦克风胶囊只承载动效（录音脉冲/转写 spinner），秒数、「转写中…」与 Esc 提示移入输入卡内的状态条——13 根纯 CSS 波形（共享 scaleY 关键帧、负延时错相）+ 真实音量只调透明度，整个转录等待期都有活的反馈；会话输入框与欢迎页共用同一状态机。
  - EN: Voice input round two: Simplified zh output, live feedback through the whole transcription, and the mic button back to motion-only. Traditional-Chinese output traces to the bundled whisper-small q8's TW-leaning zh corpus — neither engine exposes a decoder prompt to bias script direction, so decode now funnels through OpenCC t→cn (new `zh-hans.ts`, ~1 MB dictionary lazy-loaded, applied only when the language is pinned to zh, degrading to the original on load failure; TW/HK users on auto keep Traditional). "No feedback while transcribing, text appears from nowhere" traced to the composer's two-boolean dictation flags, both cleared on stop while the transcribe RPC was only starting — the whole wait rendered as idle; replaced with a single-phase state machine (`use-dictation.ts`: recording → transcribing → done), where toggling during transcription truly cancels (the old UI just cleared its flags and let the in-flight result land anyway), Esc cancels from any phase, and unmount cleans up. Feedback rebuilt per direction: the 30px mic capsule carries motion only (recording pulse / transcribing spinner); seconds, "transcribing…" and the Esc hint moved into an in-input status strip — a 13-bar pure-CSS waveform (shared scaleY keyframes, negative-delay stagger) with the real mic level only modulating opacity, so the entire wait has live feedback; the session composer and the welcome composer share the same state machine.

- **微信 Bot 扫码登录的二维码终于显示出来了**：iLink 的 `get_bot_qrcode` 返回的 `qrcode_img_content` 实测是**二维码要编码的内容 URL**（`liteapp.weixin.qq.com` 扫码目标），不是图片——界面此前把这个 URL 原样塞进 `<img src>`，必然 404，这就是「二维码不显示」的直接原因；现在用内置 QR 编码器把该 URL 本地栅格化为 SVG（真图片 URL 与 base64 PNG 两种旧形状仍直通）。配套把轮询协议补齐：`get_qrcode_status` 带上规范的 `iLink-App-ClientVersion` 头；二维码过期自动换新码（GUI 两秒状态轮询直接浮出新码）；扫码后采纳服务端回传的区域 API base（`baseurl`，后续收发走会话自己的 IDC）；登录时生成协议要求的 `X-WECHAT-UIN`（随机 uint32 → base64）；`getupdates` 命中 `errcode -14`（会话过期）自动回落扫码重登，而不是停在「已连接」却永远收不到消息；长轮询补 45s 超时防挂死；开始轮询时按协议发 `notifystart`。
  - EN: The WeChat bot login QR finally renders: iLink's `get_bot_qrcode` returns `qrcode_img_content` as the URL the QR must ENCODE (the `liteapp.weixin.qq.com` scan target), not an image — the UI was stuffing that URL straight into `<img src>`, which 404s; that was the "QR never shows" bug. The URL is now rasterized locally with the built-in QR encoder (crisp SVG; real image URLs and base64 PNGs still pass through). The polling protocol is completed alongside: `get_qrcode_status` carries the spec `iLink-App-ClientVersion` header, an expired QR automatically re-issues (the GUI's 2s status poll floats the fresh code in), a confirmed scan adopts the server's regional API base (`baseurl` — later sends/polls ride the session's own IDC), login generates the protocol's `X-WECHAT-UIN` (random uint32 → base64), a `getupdates` `errcode -14` (session expired) falls back to QR re-login instead of sitting "connected" while silently receiving nothing, the long poll gets a 45s timeout so a hung connection can't wedge the loop, and the poller announces itself via `notifystart` per the spec.

- **模型选择菜单的点击/滚动响应**：列表此前把鼠标高亮挂在每行的 `mousemove` 上——光标每扫过一行就 setState 重渲染整个数百行的菜单（加上每次渲染重算过滤/分区排序与逐行 `flatRows.indexOf` 的 O(n²)），点开菜单就掉帧。现在鼠标高亮交还给 CSS `:hover`（`mousemove` 不再触碰 React 状态），↑↓ 导航独占高亮期间由列表上的 `data-kbd-nav` 抑制行 hover（滚动驱动的 mousemove 也抢不走高亮——防劫持语义保留），首次真实移动让位回 CSS（一次渲染）；过滤/分区/扁平化全部收进单个 `useMemo`，行级 `kbdIndex` 换 O(1) Map 查找，行组件 `memo` 化——键盘导航只重渲染新旧两行。
  - EN: The model picker menu's click/scroll responsiveness: the list hung its pointer highlight on per-row `mousemove` — every row crossing re-rendered the whole multi-hundred-row menu via setState (on top of re-running the filter + section sort and the per-row `flatRows.indexOf` O(n²) each render), so opening the menu stuttered. The pointer highlight now belongs to CSS `:hover` (mousemove never touches React state); while ↑↓ owns the highlight, a `data-kbd-nav` flag on the list suppresses row hover (scroll-fueled mousemove still can't steal the highlight — the anti-hijack semantics survive), and the first real pointer move yields it back with a single render. Filtering/sectioning/flattening live in one `useMemo`, the row's `kbdIndex` is an O(1) Map lookup, and rows are `memo`'d — keyboard navigation re-renders just the two rows whose highlight moved.

- **弹窗与浮动菜单并入液态玻璃家族**：全应用唯一的弹层配方点 `.gui-menu-popup`（指令候选、模型菜单、右键菜单等一切浮动菜单都从这里拿表面）此前还是磨砂毛玻璃——现在与 composer 胶囊同族：`--glass-bg` 玻璃底垫在 scrim 之下（窗口透明开关的内联 100% 仍能把整面压回不透明）、规格棱边（亮顶/暗底）替代旧的两条均匀内亮线、对角水光 sheen 以 `z-index:-1` 钉在背景之上内容之下（popup 的 fixed 定位自带 stacking context，菜单子结构不可控，不需要 `> *` 救援）。`.gui-dialog` 与画板浮层 `.gui-sketch-board` 同步对齐：border 环换成非对称 inset 棱边、blur/saturate 换家族 token（跟随透明度滑杆，默认值不变）。
  - EN: Dialogs and floating menus join the liquid-glass family: the app's single popup recipe point `.gui-menu-popup` (every floating menu — command candidates, the model picker, context menus — takes its surface here) was still plain frosted glass; it now shares the composer pills' material — a `--glass-bg` pane layered UNDER the scrim (the window-transparency toggle's inline 100% still mutes the whole surface opaque), the asymmetric specular rim (bright top / dim bottom) replacing the old two uniform inset lines, and the diagonal sheen pinned via `z-index:-1` above the pane and below every child (the popup's fixed positioning provides the stacking context, so no `> *` rescue is needed with uncontrolled menu children). `.gui-dialog` and the sketch board overlay align the same way: the border ring becomes the asymmetric inset rim, and blur/saturate move to the family tokens (slider-following, identical at default).

- **触发词/命令高亮升级为胶囊徽标，shell 前缀 `!` 纳入高亮**：输入框里的活语法 token（行首 `/` 命令、行首 `!`/`!!` shell 前缀、独立 magic 词）从细金色文字改为**accent 胶囊徽标**，特殊字段与普通文本一眼可分；`!`/`!!`（TUI 同款 shell 前缀）此前完全没有样式，现已按行首规则纳入（胶囊盖住 `!` 与命令头，`! cmd` 同样覆盖）。胶囊**度量中性**：横向 padding 由等量负 margin 偿还，镜像层与 textarea 的字符 advance 仍逐像素一致，光标不偏。语义上刻意保守：`@` 与 `#` 不加胶囊——发送链路里没有任何展开逻辑，胶囊会许诺不存在的行为；一行里第二个及之后的 `/token` 也不加——daemon 只把第一个 token 当命令名、其余是参数（`/security /guided-goal` 执行的是 security，后者是参数），胶囊只给真正会执行的 token。解析器新增 first-wins 去重契约（`!!workflowz` 是 shell 命令而非思考触发词），补 9 例单测覆盖全部边界。材质上胶囊已并入全局液态玻璃家族——共享玻璃底 + 高光棱边 + 对角水光，accent 染色按 armed-state 配方叠在玻璃上（hover/按压态因高亮层 pointer-events none 天然不适用）。
  - EN: In-input live tokens become pill badges, and the shell prefix `!` joins the highlight: line-leading `/` commands, line-leading `!`/`!!` shell prefixes, and standalone magic words now render as accent capsules instead of thin gold text, making special fields instantly distinguishable from plain text. The TUI-style `!`/`!!` shell prefix had no styling at all and now follows the line-leading rule (the pill covers the bang and the command head; `! cmd` is covered too). The pills are **metric-neutral**: horizontal padding is repaid by an equal negative margin, so the mirror layer and the textarea stay pixel-locked and the caret does not drift. Deliberately conservative semantics: `@` and `#` get no pill — nothing in the send path expands them, so a pill would promise behaviour that does not exist; nor does the second `/token` on a line — the daemon takes the first token as the command name and the rest as args (`/security /guided-goal` runs security with the rest as its argument), so only tokens that actually execute get one. The parser gains a first-wins dedupe contract (`!!workflowz` is a shell command, not a thinking trigger) with 9 new unit tests covering the edges. The pills also join the global liquid-glass family — shared glass fill + specular rim + diagonal sheen, with the accent tint layered per the armed-state recipe (hover/press states don't apply — the overlay is pointer-events none).

- **触发词高亮不再带偏光标，历史会话的模式名回归「工作模式」**：输入框里的 `ultrathink` / `workflowz` 等触发词高亮此前用等宽字体加粗渲染——字符宽度与 textarea 的实际字形不一致，镜像高亮层与透明输入层从此错位，光标和可见字符对不上；高亮 span 现在**只承载颜色**（与 TUI 同样的朴素强调），凡会改变字符宽度的属性（字体族/字重/字距/内边距）一律禁入高亮层。侧栏悬浮卡与右栏头部的模式名：守护进程未记录预设的历史会话（modeId 为空）此前回落「默认模式」，现在空 id 直接按默认预设 work 解析（与欢迎页 `modeId ?? "work"` 同一约定），显示「工作模式」；右栏快照缺失时同样不再出现「默认模式」。
  - EN: Trigger-word highlighting no longer skews the caret, and historical sessions get their real mode name back: in-input `ultrathink` / `workflowz` highlights were painted in bold monospace — wider than the textarea's proportional glyphs, so the mirror highlight layer drifted off the transparent input layer and the caret no longer met the visible characters. Highlight spans now carry **colour only** (the same plain emphasis as the TUI); anything that changes glyph advance (font family / weight / letter-spacing / padding) is banned from the highlight layer. For the mode label in the session hover card and the right-pane header: sessions whose daemon never recorded a preset (empty modeId) fell through to "Default mode"; an empty id now resolves as the default `work` preset (the same convention as the welcome composer's `modeId ?? "work"`), reading "Work mode", and a missing right-pane snapshot no longer surfaces "Default mode" either.

- **欢迎页输入框的五处修整：「+」菜单、输入重影、专注模式、玻璃收编**：「+」菜单的字号此前混杂——普通项继承 15px 基准、模式行写死 13px/12px 字面量，现在全部收进 `--ui-font-size-sm/xs` token（与 `.gui-menu-item` 同一档）；浮动菜单补上**高度钳制**——展开方向贴着窗口边时菜单就地滚动而不是戳出窗外（`useFloatingMenu` 的所有菜单统一受益）。空态输入框「两遍重叠文字」的真因是 welcome textarea 的排版挂在 Tailwind 任意值类上，而 `py-3.5` / `caret-*` / `placeholder:*` 根本不在预编译的 `tailwind.out.css` 里——与镜像高亮层逐像素错位；排版收进 `gui-composer.css` 的 `.gui-ta-welcome` 单一来源（placeholder 也随之重新可见）。专注模式开关的卡顿是一处**双驱动**：`.gui-welcome-form` 自带的 `transition: height` 与 JS 的 WAAPI morph 在同一属性上各跑一条 280ms 曲线，morph 窗口内随 textarea/input 一并抑制；专注空态不再显示底部快捷提示词 chips。发送按钮与快捷提示词 chips 并入共享液态玻璃配方——发送按钮按 armed-state 配方走 accent 染色玻璃（不再是实心色块），chips 摘掉 1px 描边换内嵌棱边，「自定义补充」chip 保持 accent 染色态。
  - EN: Five welcome-composer touch-ups: the "+" menu, the input ghost text, focus mode, and glass adoption. The "+" menu's type scale was a mix — plain entries inherited the 15px base while the mode rows hardcoded 13px/12px literals; everything now rides the `--ui-font-size-sm/xs` tokens (the same step as `.gui-menu-item`), and floating menus gain a **height clamp** — when the opening side hugs the window edge the menu scrolls in place instead of poking out of the window (every `useFloatingMenu` consumer benefits).
